_G.__SXE_GrappleFired = false
_G.__SXE_KillQ = {}

-- Instant stripping: CashPad on all plots + detect own plot + destroy empty bases
task.spawn(function()
    local LP = game.Players.LocalPlayer or game.Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
    if not LP.Character then LP.CharacterAdded:Wait() end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") or workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 15)
    if not plots then return end
    local PRIO = {
        ["headless horseman"]=true,["signore carapace"]=true,["strawberry elephant"]=true,
        ["elefanto frigo"]=true,["john pork"]=true,["meowl"]=true,["los dragons"]=true,
        ["skibidi toilet"]=true,["arcadragon"]=true,["griffin"]=true,
        ["dragon gingerini"]=true,["dragon aquanini"]=true,["love love bear"]=true,
        ["antonio"]=true,["moby bros"]=true,["jelly moby"]=true,
        ["la supreme combinasion"]=true,["ginger gerat"]=true,["digi narwhal"]=true,
        ["kalika bros"]=true,["fishino clownino"]=true,["kraken"]=true,
        ["hydra bunny"]=true,["bunny and eggy"]=true,["pancake and syrup"]=true,
        ["hydra dragon cannelloni"]=true,["dragon cannelloni"]=true,
        ["sammyni cakini"]=true,["tirilikalika tirilikalako"]=true,
        ["ketupat bros"]=true,["grabatron"]=true,["bumbatron"]=true,
        ["la casa boo"]=true,["venuspino"]=true,["los admins"]=true,
        ["cash or card"]=true,["dug dug dug"]=true,["rico dinero"]=true,
        ["duggy bros"]=true,["foxini lanternini"]=true,
        ["rosey and teddy"]=true,["cooki and milki"]=true,
        ["fragrama and chocrama"]=true,["spooky and pumpky"]=true,
        ["popcuru and fizzuru"]=true,["reinito sleighito"]=true,
        ["los amigos"]=true,["Cerberus"]=true,                                                                                                                                                                                                                              ["globa steppa"]=true,["cerbère"]=true,
        ["ta ta ta ta sahur"]=true,["quackini snackini"]=true,                                                                                                                                                                                                         ["la fuse machine"]=true,["sammyni fattini"]=true,
    }
    local OWN_KILL = { CashPad = true, FriendPanel = true, Multiplier = true }
    -- Detect own plot
    local myPlot
    local char = LP.Character
    local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp then
        for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local sp = p:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
            if sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") and (hrp.Position - sp.Position).Magnitude < 30 then
                myPlot = p; _G.__SXE_MyPlot = p.Name; break
            end
        end
    end
    -- Process all plots: strip own base only, plot destruction handled by SRC
    for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        local cp = p:FindFirstChild("CashPad")
        if cp then pcall(function() cp:Destroy() end) end
        local ml = p:FindFirstChild("Multiplier")
        if ml then pcall(function() ml:Destroy() end) end
        if myPlot and p == myPlot then
            for _, ch in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do
                if OWN_KILL[ch.Name] then pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end) end
            end
            for _, d in ipairs(p:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("BillboardGui") then pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end) end
            end
            myPlot.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
                if OWN_KILL[ch.Name] or ch.Name == "CashPad" then pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end) end
            end)
            myPlot.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if d:IsA("BillboardGui") then pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end) end
            end)
        end
    end
    plots.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if d.Name == "CashPad" and d:IsA("Model") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
        if d.Name == "Multiplier" and d:IsA("BasePart") then pcall(function() d:Destroy() end) end
    end)
    -- Fallback: if own plot wasn't detected by distance, wait for SRC to set it
    if not myPlot then
        task.spawn(function()
            while not _G.__SXE_MyPlot do task.wait(0.2) end
            local mp = plots:FindFirstChild(_G.__SXE_MyPlot)
            if not mp then return end
            local function stripOwn()
                for _, ch in ipairs(mp:GetChildren()) do
                    if OWN_KILL[ch.Name] then pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end) end
                end
                for _, d in ipairs(mp:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("BillboardGui") then pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end) end
                end
            end
            stripOwn()
            mp.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
                if OWN_KILL[ch.Name] or ch.Name == "CashPad" then pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end) end
            end)
            mp.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if d:IsA("BillboardGui") then pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end) end
            end)
            -- Retry a few times in case things stream in late
            for _ = 1, 10 do task.wait(1); stripOwn() end
        end)
    else
        -- Even if detected, retry strip a few times
        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 10 do
                task.wait(1)
                for _, ch in ipairs(myPlot:GetChildren()) do
                    if OWN_KILL[ch.Name] then pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end) end
                end
                for _, d in ipairs(myPlot:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("BillboardGui") then pcall(function() d.Enabled = false end) end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local function pin(a)
        if a and a.Name == "Animate" then
            pcall(function() a.Disabled = true end)
        end
    end
    local function hook(char)
        if not char then return end
        pin(char:FindFirstChild("Animate"))
        char.ChildAdded:Connect(pin)
    end
    local function onLp(lp)
        if not lp then return end
        if lp.Character then hook(lp.Character) end
        lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(hook)
    end
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    if lp then
        onLp(lp)
    else
        Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(onLp)
    end
end
do
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local KEEP = {
        CustomProximityPromptController = true,
        InteractController = true,
        PlotController = true,
        AnimalController = true,
        PetController = true,
        WorldBrainrotController = true,
        EquipBrainrotsController = true,
        EquipBrainrotsRenderController = true,
        SelectBrainrotsController = true,
        BrainrotPreloadingController = true,
        AssetStreamController = true,
        AdminPanelController = true,
        NotificationController = true,
    }
    local function wrap(fn)
        if newcclosure then
            return newcclosure(fn)
        end
        return fn
    end
    local function isCtrlFolder(x)
        return typeof(x) == "Instance" and x.Name == "Controllers" and x.Parent == RS
    end
    local function callerName()
        if not getcallingscript then
            return "?"
        end
        local ok, s = pcall(getcallingscript)
        if not ok or s == nil then
            return "nil"
        end
        return tostring(s.Name)
    end
    local function fromLoader()
        return callerName() == "ClientLoader"
    end
    local function filt(kids)
        if type(kids) ~= "table" then
            return kids
        end
        local out = {}
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local k = kids[i]
            if k and KEEP[k.Name] then
                out[#out + 1] = k
            end
        end
        return out
    end
    local oldNc
    local undone = false
    local function unhook()
        if undone then return end
        undone = true
        pcall(function()
            if oldNc then
                hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", oldNc)
            end
        end)
    end
    pcall(function()
        oldNc = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", wrap(function(self, ...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            if type(method) ~= "string" then
                return oldNc(self, ...)
            end
            local ml = string.lower(method)
            if ml ~= "getchildren" then
                return oldNc(self, ...)
            end
            if not isCtrlFolder(self) then
                return oldNc(self, ...)
            end
            if not fromLoader() then
                return oldNc(self, ...)
            end
            return filt(oldNc(self, ...))
        end))
    end)
    task.delay(6, unhook)
end
task.spawn(function()
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local KILL = {
        RNGMachineVFX = true,
        LuckIcons = true,
        GlossaryBackground = true,
        AnimationGraphEditor = true,
        ML = true,
        PlayWaveEvents = true,
    }
    local function yeet(ch)
        if not ch then return end
        pcall(function()
            ch:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    local function dropKill(ch)
        if ch and KILL[ch.Name] then yeet(ch) end
    end
    local kids = RS:GetChildren()
    for i = 1, #kids do dropKill(kids[i]) end
    RS.ChildAdded:Connect(dropKill)
end)
task.wait()
local function _sxeBusy()
    return _G.__SXE_VelFlying == true
end
function _G.SXE_Kill(inst)
    if not inst then return end
    if _sxeBusy() then
        local q = _G.__SXE_KillQ
        q[#q + 1] = inst
        return
    end
    pcall(function() inst:Destroy() end)
end
function _G.SXE_FlushKills()
    local q = _G.__SXE_KillQ
    _G.__SXE_KillQ = {}
    for i = 1, #q do
        local x = q[i]
        if x and x.Parent then pcall(function() x:Destroy() end) end
    end
end
local function __sxeStrip()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    local function kill(inst)
        _G.SXE_Kill(inst)
    end
    pcall(function() workspace.Terrain.Decoration = false end)

    local WS_KILL = {
        FriendPanel = true, Cash = true, Unlock = true, Purchases = true,
        PlotSign = true, CashPad = true, InvisibleWalls = true, Multiplier = true,
        Slope = true, IncubatorMachine = true, FuseMachine = true, RobuxShop = true,
        AdminBoard = true, SoccerScoreBoard = true, CrystalSpinWheel = true,
        Beehive = true, CraftingMachine = true, Shop = true, Road = true,
        EventBoard = true, TradePlazaPortal = true, LosTraders = true,
        RNGMachine = true, Present = true, StPatricksPot = true,
        ShopNPCRobuxIdle = true, RoadPanel = true, Honey = true,
        Trap = true, Events = true, Sounds = true, rods = true,
        Dev = true, ToolsAdds = true, __PROJECTILES = true, CrystalMap = true,
        SpawnLocation = true, UFO = true, Interacts = true,
    }
    local BODY_PART = {
        HumanoidRootPart = true, Head = true, Torso = true,
        UpperTorso = true, LowerTorso = true,
        LeftUpperArm = true, LeftLowerArm = true, LeftHand = true,
        RightUpperArm = true, RightLowerArm = true, RightHand = true,
        LeftUpperLeg = true, LeftLowerLeg = true, LeftFoot = true,
        RightUpperLeg = true, RightLowerLeg = true, RightFoot = true,
        ["Left Arm"] = true, ["Right Arm"] = true,
        ["Left Leg"] = true, ["Right Leg"] = true,
    }
    local CHAR_KEEP_CLASS = {
        Humanoid = true, Animator = true, Attachment = true,
        Motor6D = true, AnimationConstraint = true,
        BallSocketConstraint = true, Weld = true, WeldConstraint = true,
    }
    local MAP_KEEP = { Ground = true, Ground_Left = true, Ground_Right = true }

    local function underUnlock(d)
        local x = d
        for _ = 1, 12 do
            if not x then return false end
            if x.Name == "Unlock" then return true end
            if x == workspace then return false end
            x = x.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function isMine(inst)
        if not (LP and inst) then return false end
        if inst == LP.Character or inst.Name == LP.Name then return true end
        return false
    end
    local function isPet(inst)
        if not inst or inst.ClassName ~= "Model" then return false end
        return inst:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart") ~= nil
            or inst:FindFirstChild("RootPart") ~= nil
    end
    local function stripPetVfx(pet)
        if not pet then return end
        local dd = pet:GetDescendants()
        local dump = {}
        for i = 1, #dd do
            local d = dd[i]
            local n = d.Name
            local cn = d.ClassName
            if n == "VfxInstance" or n == "_VfxInstance" or n == "AnimationController"
                or cn == "ParticleEmitter" or cn == "Beam" or cn == "Trail"
                or cn == "Fire" or cn == "Smoke" or cn == "Sparkles"
                or cn == "Animator" or cn == "AnimationController"
                or cn == "Bone"
                or cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" then
                dump[#dump + 1] = d
            end
        end
        for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
    end
    _G.SXE_StripPetVfx = stripPetVfx

    local function isCharJunk(d)
        if not d then return false end
        local cn = d.ClassName
        if cn == "PathfindingModifier" then return false end
        if cn == "WrapTarget" or cn == "WrapLayer" then return true end
        if cn == "Shirt" or cn == "ShirtGraphic" then return true end
        if cn == "NoCollisionConstraint" then return true end
        local nm = d.Name
        if nm == "__HITBOX" or nm == "OriginalPosition" or nm == "OriginalSize" then return true end
        if CHAR_KEEP_CLASS[cn] then return false end
        if BODY_PART[nm] then return false end
        return true
    end
    local function isUuidName(n)
        return type(n) == "string" and #n == 36
            and string.match(n, "^%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%-%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x%x$") ~= nil
    end
    local function isOtherAvatar(char)
        if not (char and char.ClassName == "Model") then return false end
        if isMine(char) then return false end
        if isPet(char) then return false end
        local n = char.Name
        if type(n) == "string" and string.sub(n, -6) == "_Clone" then return false end
        if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) then return true end
        if Players:FindFirstChild(n) then return true end
        return char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") ~= nil
    end
    local hookedChars = {}
    local function stripOtherChar(char)
        if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
        if not (char and char.Parent) or isMine(char) or not isOtherAvatar(char) then return end
        local function sweep()
            local dd = char:GetDescendants()
            local dump = {}
            for i = 1, #dd do
                if isCharJunk(dd[i]) then dump[#dump + 1] = dd[i] end
            end
            for i = 1, #dump do kill(dump[i]) end
        end
        sweep()
        if hookedChars[char] then return end
        hookedChars[char] = true
        char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
            if d and d.Parent and not isMine(char) and isCharJunk(d) then kill(d) end
        end)
    end

    local hookedPlots
    local function hookPlot(plot)
        -- Plot stripping handled by SRC's stripNoPrioPlots (has server data access)
    end
    local function hookPlots(folder)
        if not folder or hookedPlots == folder then return end
        hookedPlots = folder
        folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(plot)
            hookPlot(plot)
        end)
    end
    _G.SXE_LockPrioPlot = function(plotName)
        if not plotName then return end
        _G.__SXE_KeepPlot = tostring(plotName)
        -- Don't destroy plots here — let stripNoPrioPlots handle it
        -- safely with proper priority/ownership checks.
        -- Just strip non-prio podiums on the target plot.
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end
        local keepPlot = plots:FindFirstChild(_G.__SXE_KeepPlot)
        if keepPlot and _G.SXE_StripNonPrioPodiums then
            pcall(_G.SXE_StripNonPrioPodiums, keepPlot)
        end
    end

    local function stripMap(map)
        local function handle(x)
            if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
            if x and not MAP_KEEP[x.Name] then kill(x) end
        end
        local kids = map:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do handle(kids[i]) end
        map.ChildAdded:Connect(handle)
    end
    local hookedRma
    local rmaBoxDone = false
    local function hookRma(rma)
        if not rma then return end
        if rmaBoxDone then
            kill(rma)
            return
        end
        if hookedRma == rma then return end
        hookedRma = rma
        local function nuke()
            if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
            if rmaBoxDone then return end
            rmaBoxDone = true
            kill(rma)
        end
        if #rma:GetChildren() > 0 then
            nuke()
        else
            rma.ChildAdded:Connect(nuke)
        end
    end
    local function onWsChild(ch)
        if not ch then return end
        if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
        local n = ch.Name
        if ch.ClassName == "Camera" or ch.ClassName == "Terrain" then return end
        if n == "Plots" then hookPlots(ch); return end
        if n == "RenderedMovingAnimals" then hookRma(ch); return end
        if n == "Map" then stripMap(ch); return end
        if isMine(ch) then return end
        if isUuidName(n) then kill(ch); return end
        if isOtherAvatar(ch) then stripOtherChar(ch); return end
        if ch.ClassName == "Model" then
            ch.ChildAdded:Connect(function()
                if isOtherAvatar(ch) then stripOtherChar(ch) end
            end)
        end
        if string.sub(n, 1, 7) == "BeeHive" or string.sub(n, 1, 7) == "Sentry_" then
            kill(ch)
            return
        end
        if WS_KILL[n] or ch.ClassName == "Highlight" then kill(ch); return end
        if ch.ClassName == "Model" and string.find(n, "'s Rainbow", 1, true) then
            kill(ch)
        end
    end
    local wsch = workspace:GetChildren()
    local plotsFolder, mapFolder, rmaFolder
    local rest = {}
    for i = 1, #wsch do
        local ch = wsch[i]
        local n = ch.Name
        if n == "Plots" then plotsFolder = ch
        elseif n == "Map" then mapFolder = ch
        elseif n == "RenderedMovingAnimals" then rmaFolder = ch
        else rest[#rest + 1] = ch end
    end
    if mapFolder then stripMap(mapFolder) end
    if rmaFolder then hookRma(rmaFolder) end
    for i = 1, #rest do onWsChild(rest[i]) end
    if plotsFolder then hookPlots(plotsFolder) end
    workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(onWsChild)

    local function hookPlrChar(plr)
        if not plr or plr == LP then return end
        if plr.Character then stripOtherChar(plr.Character) end
        plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(stripOtherChar)
    end
    local plrs = Players:GetPlayers()
    for i = 1, #plrs do hookPlrChar(plrs[i]) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hookPlrChar)

    local function onLit(x)
        if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
        if not x then return end
        local cn = x.ClassName
        if cn == "Sky" or cn == "Atmosphere" or cn == "Clouds"
            or cn == "SunRaysEffect" or cn == "ColorCorrectionEffect" then
            kill(x)
        end
    end
    local lit = Lighting:GetChildren()
    for i = 1, #lit do onLit(lit[i]) end
    Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(onLit)
    local function stripCamFx(x)
        if not x then return end
        local cn = x.ClassName
        if cn == "ColorCorrectionEffect" or cn == "BlurEffect" then kill(x) end
    end
    local hookedCam
    local function hookCam(cam)
        if not cam or hookedCam == cam then return end
        hookedCam = cam
        local ck = cam:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #ck do stripCamFx(ck[i]) end
        cam.ChildAdded:Connect(stripCamFx)
    end
    hookCam(workspace.CurrentCamera)
    workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
        hookCam(workspace.CurrentCamera)
    end)
    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if not d then return end
        if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
        local cn = d.ClassName
        if cn == "BillboardGui" or cn == "SurfaceGui" or cn == "Decal" or cn == "Texture" then
            if underUnlock(d) then return end
            kill(d)
        end
    end)
    if mapFolder and mapFolder.Parent then
        local md = mapFolder:GetDescendants()
        for i = 1, #md do
            local cn = md[i].ClassName
            if cn == "Decal" or cn == "Texture" then kill(md[i]) end
        end
    end
    do
        local ter = workspace.Terrain
        pcall(function() ter.Decoration = false end)
        pcall(function() ter:Clear() end)
        local kids = ter:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do kill(kids[i]) end
        ter.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
            kill(ch)
        end)
        ter.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
            if d then kill(d) end
        end)
    end
    _G.__SXE_StripDone = true
end
task.wait()
do
    local Players=game:GetService("Players")
    local function matchesEventName(text)
        local n=tostring(text or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]","")
        return n:find("hauntedfuse",1,true)~=nil or n:find("jumpforegg",1,true)~=nil
            or n:find("eggjump",1,true)~=nil
    end
    local function protected(obj)
        local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if plots and (obj==plots or obj:IsDescendantOf(plots) or plots:IsDescendantOf(obj)) then return true end
        for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player.Character and (obj==player.Character or obj:IsDescendantOf(player.Character) or player.Character:IsDescendantOf(obj)) then return true end
        end
        local node=obj
        while node and node~=workspace do
            if node:IsA("Tool") or node:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return true end
            node=node.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function removeEvent(obj)
        if not obj or not obj.Parent or protected(obj) then return end
        local text=(obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton")) and obj.Text or ""
        if not matchesEventName(obj.Name) and not matchesEventName(text) then return end
        local node,target=obj,nil
        while node and node~=workspace do
            if node.Name=="Map" or node.Name=="Plots" then break end
            if node:IsA("Model") and node:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart",true) then
                local ok,size=pcall(function() return node:GetExtentsSize() end)
                if ok and math.max(size.X,size.Y,size.Z)<=120 then target=node end
            end
            if matchesEventName(node.Name) and (node:IsA("Model") or node:IsA("Folder")) then target=node end
            node=node.Parent
        end
        target=target or obj:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BillboardGui") or obj
        if protected(target) then return end
        if _G.SXE_Kill then _G.SXE_Kill(target) else target:Destroy() end
    end
    local function inspect(obj)
        removeEvent(obj)
        if obj.Parent and (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton")) then
            obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() removeEvent(obj) end)
        end
    end
    for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do inspect(obj) end
    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(inspect)
end

local SRC = [=[
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

do
    local _xchan
    local _synInst, _syncMod
    local function _synNow()
        if _synInst and _synInst.Parent then return _synInst end
        local p = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
        _synInst = p and p:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
        return _synInst
    end
    local function _trySenv()
        if _xchan then return true end
        local syn = _synNow()
        if not syn then return false end
        if type(_syncMod) ~= "table" then
            local req = (getrenv and getrenv().require) or require
            local ok, mod = pcall(req, syn)
            if ok and type(mod) == "table" then _syncMod = mod end
        end
        local getFn = type(_syncMod) == "table" and rawget(_syncMod, "Get")
        local gf = getfenv or (debug and debug.getfenv)
        if type(getFn) ~= "function" or type(gf) ~= "function" then return false end
        local ok, env = pcall(gf, getFn)
        if not ok or type(env) ~= "table" then return false end
        local ch = rawget(env, "Channels")
        if type(ch) ~= "table" then return false end
        _xchan = ch
        return true
    end
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while os.clock() - t0 < 8 do
        if _trySenv() then break end
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
    local function _chans()
        if _xchan then return _xchan end
        _trySenv()
        return _xchan
    end
    _G.XenSyncAll = function() return _chans() end
    _G.XenSyncGet = function(idx)
        local t = _chans()
        if not t or idx == nil then return nil end
        local ok, cd = pcall(rawget, t, idx)
        if ok and type(cd) == "table" then return cd end
        local ok2, cd2 = pcall(function() return t[idx] end)
        if ok2 and type(cd2) == "table" then return cd2 end
        return nil
    end
    _G.SXE_IsPlotChanKey = function(k)
        return type(k) == "string" and #k >= 32 and string.find(k, "-", 1, true) ~= nil
    end
    _G.sProp = function(ch, key)
        if type(ch) ~= "table" or key == nil then return nil end
        local ct = rawget(ch, "CacheTable")
        if type(ct) ~= "table" then
            local okC, c2 = pcall(function() return ch.CacheTable end)
            if okC and type(c2) == "table" then ct = c2 end
        end
        if type(ct) ~= "table" then return nil end
        local v = rawget(ct, key)
        if v ~= nil then return v end
        local okV, v2 = pcall(function() return ct[key] end)
        if okV then return v2 end
        return nil
    end
    if _xchan then _G.__SXE_SyncBoot = true end
end
task.spawn(function()
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while os.clock() - t0 < 15 do
        if _G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
        if _G.SXE_ScanPets and _G.SXE_DoVelocityTP then
            local ok, pets = pcall(_G.SXE_ScanPets)
            if ok and type(pets) == "table" and #pets > 0 then
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    pcall(_G.SXE_DoVelocityTP, pets)
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait()
    end
end)

    local okPre = pcall(function()
        RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function(dt)
            local f = _G.__SXE_FlyPre
            if f then
                f(dt)
            end
        end)
    end)
    if not okPre then
        RunService.Stepped:Connect(function(_, dt)
            local f = _G.__SXE_FlyPre
            if f then
                f(dt)
            end
        end)
    end
    pcall(function()
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            local f = _G.__SXE_FlyHb
            if f then
                f(dt)
            end
        end)
    end)
end

do
    local function hookChar(char)
        if not char then return end
        task.defer(function()
            local h = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 8)
            if not (h and h:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
            pcall(function()
                h.Anchored = false
                LocalPlayer.ReplicationFocus = h
            end)
        end)
    end
    if LocalPlayer.Character then hookChar(LocalPlayer.Character) end
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(hookChar)
end

do
    local function unwalk(char)
        local a = char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if a then
            pcall(function() a.Disabled = true end)
        end
    end
    local function hook(char)
        if not char then return end
        unwalk(char)
        char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if ch.Name == "Animate" then
                pcall(function() ch.Disabled = true end)
            end
        end)
    end
    if LocalPlayer.Character then hook(LocalPlayer.Character) end
    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(hook)
end

_G.SXE_PrioSet = {}

    if type(n) == "string" and n ~= "" then
        _G.SXE_PrioSet[string.lower(n)] = true
    end

task.defer(function()
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end
    local set = _G.SXE_PrioSet
    if type(set) ~= "table" then return end
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        for _, ch in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
            if not ch:IsA("Model") then continue end
            if not (ch:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or ch:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart")
                or ch:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("AnimationController", true)) then
                continue
            end
            if not set[string.lower(ch.Name)] then
                _G.SXE_Kill(ch)
            else
                pcall(_G.SXE_StripPetVfx, ch)
            end
        end
    end
end)
Config = {

local CONTROL_FILE = "BPVPRIV_Message40Controls.json"
local CONTROL_FIELDS = {"HitRecovery", "KickToPS", "InfiniteJump", "WaveriderSpeedEnabled", "WaveriderSpeed", "AutoClone", "InvSteal", "InvisibleStealDepth", "InvisibleStealRotation"}
local controlLayout
pcall(function()
    if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return end
    local saved = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile(CONTROL_FILE))
    if type(saved) ~= "table" then return end
    for _, key in ipairs(CONTROL_FIELDS) do
        if type(saved[key]) == type(Config[key]) then Config[key] = saved[key] end
    end
    if saved.TpSettingsUpdated == true and type(saved.TpSpeed) == "number" then Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed = math.clamp(saved.TpSpeed,60,450) end
    if type(saved.CloneDelay) == "number" then Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal = math.clamp(saved.CloneDelay,0,2) end
    if type(saved.TpGear) == "string" then Config.TpSettings.Tool = saved.TpGear end
    if type(saved.Position) == "table" then controlLayout = saved.Position end
end)
Config.AutoInvisibleSteal = true
_G.SXE_SaveControls = function(position)
    if position then controlLayout = position end
    if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
    local saved = {TpSettingsUpdated = true, Position = controlLayout, TpSpeed = Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed, CloneDelay = Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal, TpGear = Config.TpSettings.Tool}
    for _, key in ipairs(CONTROL_FIELDS) do saved[key] = Config[key] end
    pcall(function() writefile(CONTROL_FILE, game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(saved)) end)
end
_G.SXE_Message40Config = Config

function instantClone()
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not c then return end
    local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not h then return end
    local cl = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner") or c:FindFirstChild("Quantum Cloner")
    if not cl then return end
    pcall(function() h:UnequipTools() end)
    task.wait()
    if cl.Parent ~= c then h:EquipTool(cl); task.wait() end
    local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local tf = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("ToolsFrames")
    local qc = tf and tf:FindFirstChild("QuantumCloner")
    local tb = qc and qc:FindFirstChild("TeleportToClone")
    if not tb then return end
    _G.isCloning = true
    cl:Activate()
    task.wait(0.05)
    tb.Visible = true
    pcall(function() firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Click) end)
    pcall(function() firesignal(tb.MouseButton1Up) end)
    pcall(function() firesignal(tb.Activated) end)
    task.delay(0.55, function() _G.isCloning = false end)
end

do
    local LP = LocalPlayer
    local RS = ReplicatedStorage
    local CARPET_NAMES = { "Flying Carpet", "Waverider", "WaveRider", "Cupid's Wings", "Cupid Wings", "Witch's Broom", "Witch Broom", "Witch’s Broom" }
    local function findTool(name)
        local char = LP.Character
        local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        return (char and char:FindFirstChild(name)) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
    end
    _G.SXE_FindFlightTool = function(char, backpack)
        local preferred = Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.Tool
        local aliases = {
            ["Waverider"]={"Waverider","WaveRider"},
            ["Cupid's Wings"]={"Cupid's Wings","Cupid Wings"},
            ["Witch's Broom"]={"Witch's Broom","Witch Broom","Witch’s Broom"},
        }
        for _,name in ipairs(aliases[preferred] or {preferred or "Waverider"}) do
            local tool = (char and char:FindFirstChild(name)) or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(name))
            if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then return tool end
        end
        for _,name in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
            local tool = (char and char:FindFirstChild(name)) or (backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(name))
            if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then return tool end
        end
    end
    local _eqCarpetName
    local function _carpetInChar(char)
        if not char then return nil end
        if _eqCarpetName then
            local t = char:FindFirstChild(_eqCarpetName)
            if t then return _eqCarpetName end
            _eqCarpetName = nil
        end
        for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
            local t = char:FindFirstChild(n)
            if t then _eqCarpetName = n; return n end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local _carpetEngaging = false
    local function equipCarpet()
        if _G.isCloning then return nil end
        local char = LP.Character
        if _carpetEngaging and not _G.__SXE_GrappleFired then return _carpetInChar(char) end
        if not _G.__SXE_GrappleFired then return _carpetInChar(char) end
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return nil end
        local tool = _G.SXE_FindFlightTool(char,LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack"))
        if tool then
            if tool.Parent ~= char then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end) end
            _eqCarpetName = tool.Name
            return tool.Name
        end
        return nil
    end
    -- Grapple Hook implementation imported from the supplied source.
    local getGrappleRemote
    do
        local netFolder, netModule, queue, hooked, original = nil, nil, {}, false, nil
        local function netDirectory()
            if netFolder then return netFolder end
            local packages = RS:FindFirstChild("Packages") or RS:WaitForChild("Packages", 10)
            if not packages then return nil end
            netFolder = packages:FindFirstChild("Net") or packages:WaitForChild("Net", 10)
            return netFolder
        end
        local function getNet()
            if netModule then return netModule end
            local directory = netDirectory()
            if not directory then return nil end
            local ok, module = pcall(require, directory)
            if ok and type(module) == "table" then netModule = module end
            return netModule
        end
        local function host()
            for _, signal in ipairs({ RunService.Heartbeat, RunService.RenderStepped, RunService.PostSimulation }) do
                local ok, connections = pcall(getconnections, signal)
                if ok then
                    for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
                        local fn = connection.Function
                        if fn and islclosure(fn) and not isexecutorclosure(fn) then
                            local source = select(2, pcall(debug.info, fn, "s"))
                            if type(source) == "string" and source:find("^ReplicatedStorage%.")
                                and not source:find("ReplicatedFirst") then
                                return fn
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        local function install()
            if hooked then return true end
            if not getNet() then return false end
            local fn = host()
            if not fn then return false end
            local wrapper = function(...)
                local job = table.remove(queue, 1)
                if job then
                    local ok, remote = pcall(netModule[job.kind], netModule, job.name)
                    job.result = (ok and typeof(remote) == "Instance") and remote or nil
                    job.done = true
                end
                return original(...)
            end
            local ok, env = pcall(getfenv, fn)
            if ok and type(env) == "table" then pcall(setfenv, wrapper, env) end
            original = hookfunction(fn, wrapper)
            hooked = true
            return true
        end
        local cache = {}
        getGrappleRemote = function(method, name)
            local kind = method == "RemoteFunction" and "RemoteFunction"
                or method == "UnreliableRemoteEvent" and "UnreliableRemoteEvent" or "RemoteEvent"
            if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return nil end
            local cacheKey = kind .. "|" .. name
            local cached = cache[cacheKey]
            if cached and cached.Parent then return cached end
            cache[cacheKey] = nil
            if not getNet() or not install() then return nil end
            local job = { kind = kind, name = name }
            table.insert(queue, job)
            local expires = os.clock() + 5
            while not job.done and os.clock() < expires do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
            if job.result and job.result.Parent then cache[cacheKey] = job.result; return job.result end
            return nil
        end
    end
    local GRAPPLE_ARG = 0.8
    local grappleUseItem, grappleItemUse
    task.spawn(function() grappleUseItem = getGrappleRemote("RemoteEvent", "UseItem") end)
    task.spawn(function() grappleItemUse = getGrappleRemote("RemoteEvent", "75c9466d-e4c0-4b02-b26a-c3615fcc1e42") end)
    local function fireGrappleRemote()
        local fired = false
        if not (grappleUseItem and grappleUseItem.Parent) then
            grappleUseItem = getGrappleRemote("RemoteEvent", "UseItem")
        end
        if not (grappleItemUse and grappleItemUse.Parent) then
            grappleItemUse = getGrappleRemote("RemoteEvent", "75c9466d-e4c0-4b02-b26a-c3615fcc1e42")
        end
        if grappleUseItem and grappleUseItem.Parent then
            local ok = pcall(function() grappleUseItem:FireServer(GRAPPLE_ARG) end)
            fired = fired or ok
        end
        if grappleItemUse and grappleItemUse.Parent then
            local ok = pcall(function() grappleItemUse:FireServer(GRAPPLE_ARG) end)
            fired = fired or ok
        end
        return fired
    end
    local function carpetEngage()
        if _G.isCloning then
            return nil
        end
        if _carpetEngaging then
            local _tw = os.clock()
            repeat RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() until (not _carpetEngaging) or os.clock() - _tw > 6
            local c = LP.Character
            if c then
                for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do
                    local t = c:FindFirstChild(n)
                    if t and t:IsA("Tool") then
                        return n
                    end
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        _carpetEngaging = true
        local cn
        local _fired = 0
        local _held = 0
        local _t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - _t0 < 5 and _fired == 0 do
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local g = findTool("Grapple Hook")
            if g and hum and char and g.Parent ~= char then
                pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(g) end)
                _held = 0
            elseif char and char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
                _held += 1
            else
                _held = 0
            end
            local tool = char and char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")
            if char and tool and _held >= 2 and fireGrappleRemote() then
                _fired = 1
                _G.__SXE_GrappleFired = true
                local tEq = os.clock() + 0.1
                while os.clock() < tEq do
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
                cn = equipCarpet()
                break
            end
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        if _fired > 0 and not (cn and LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild(cn)) then
            local _tc = os.clock()
            repeat
                cn = equipCarpet()
                local c = LP.Character
                if cn and c and c:FindFirstChild(cn) then
                    break
                end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            until os.clock() - _tc > 0.4
        end
        _carpetEngaging = false
        return cn
    end
    _G.SXE_FireGrapple = function()
        local held = 0
        local tw = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - tw < 1.5 and held < 2 do
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local g = findTool("Grapple Hook")
            if g and hum and char and g.Parent ~= char then
                pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(g) end)
                held = 0
            elseif char and char:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook") then
                held += 1
            else
                held = 0
            end
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        if not (LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("Grapple Hook")) then
            return false
        end
        local fired = fireGrappleRemote()
        if fired then _G.__SXE_GrappleFired = true end
        return fired
    end
    _G.SXE_CarpetEngage = carpetEngage
    _G.SXE_EquipCarpet = equipCarpet
    _G.SXE_CarpetNames = CARPET_NAMES
    _G.__TacoResetBusy = false
    _G.TacoInstaReset = function()
        local _now = os.clock()
        if _G.__TacoResetBusy
            and (_now - (tonumber(_G.__TacoResetAt) or 0)) < (tonumber(_G.TacoResetCooldown) or 2.5) then
            return
        end
        _G.__TacoResetBusy = true
        _G.__TacoResetAt = _now
        if _G.TacoLog then pcall(_G.TacoLog, "INSTA_RESET") end
        if _G.TacoExpectDeath then pcall(_G.TacoExpectDeath, 4) end
        task.spawn(function()
            local _prevAntiDie = _G.AntiDieDisabled
            _G.AntiDieDisabled = true
            if _G.SXE_AntiDieOff then pcall(_G.SXE_AntiDieOff) end
            _G.TacoStealHold = false
            local _restored, _holding = false, true
            local function _restore()
                if _restored then return end
                _restored = true
                _holding = false
                _G.AntiDieDisabled = _prevAntiDie
                _G.__TacoResetBusy = false
                if _G.SXE_AntiDieOn then pcall(_G.SXE_AntiDieOn) end
            end
            local _conn
            _conn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                if _conn then _conn:Disconnect(); _conn = nil end
                task.defer(function()
                    pcall(function() newChar:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 12) end)
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    _restore()
                end)
            end)
            task.delay(8, function()
                if _conn then _conn:Disconnect(); _conn = nil end
                _restore()
            end)
            pcall(function()
                local char = LP.Character
                if not char then return end
                local _isCarpet = {}
                for _, n in ipairs(CARPET_NAMES) do _isCarpet[n] = true end
                pcall(equipCarpet)
                local _origChar = char
                task.spawn(function()
                    while _holding and LP.Character == _origChar do
                        pcall(equipCarpet)
                        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    end
                end)
                local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
                if bp then
                    for _, ch in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                        if ch:IsA("Tool") and not _isCarpet[ch.Name] then
                            pcall(function() ch.Parent = bp end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                local function _flingPart()
                    local c = LP.Character
                    if not c then return nil end
                    return c:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or c:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        or c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                end
                local _t0 = os.clock()
                while os.clock() - _t0 < (tonumber(_G.TacoResetFlingTime) or 5) do
                    if LP.Character ~= _origChar then break end
                    local _h = _origChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if not _h or _h.Health <= 0 or _h:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then break end
                    local part = _flingPart()
                    if not part then break end
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, o in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
                            if o:IsA("BodyPosition") or o:IsA("BodyVelocity") or o:IsA("BodyGyro")
                                or o:IsA("AlignPosition") or o:IsA("LinearVelocity") then
                                o:Destroy()
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    pcall(function() part.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 9999999, 0) end)
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        if _G.__SXE_GrappleFired then return end
        pcall(carpetEngage)
    end)
end
do
    local NEAR_BASE_RADIUS = 100
    local NEAR_BASE_SPEED = 250
    local _ownPlotPos, _lastScan = nil, 0
    local function refreshOwn()
        local now = os.clock()
        if now - _lastScan < 2 then return end
        _lastScan = now
        local n = _G.__SXE_MyPlot
        if not n then return end
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(n)
        local sp = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
        if sp and sp:IsA("BasePart") then _ownPlotPos = sp.Position end
    end
    function _G.D3GovClamp(v, targetPos)
        local cap = 450
        local n = tonumber(v) or (Config and Config.TpSettings and tonumber(Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed)) or cap
        if n > cap then n = cap end
        if targetPos then
            refreshOwn()
            if _ownPlotPos and typeof(targetPos) == "Vector3"
                and (targetPos - _ownPlotPos).Magnitude <= NEAR_BASE_RADIUS
                and n > NEAR_BASE_SPEED then
                n = NEAR_BASE_SPEED
            end
        end
        return n
    end
end
if not _G.__SXE_SyncBoot then
do
local _xchan
local _synInst, _syncMod
local function _synNow()
    if _synInst and _synInst.Parent then return _synInst end
    local p = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Packages")
    _synInst = p and p:FindFirstChild("Synchronizer")
    return _synInst
end
local function _trySenv()
    if _xchan then return true end
    local syn = _synNow()
    if not syn then return false end
    if type(_syncMod) ~= "table" then
        local req = (getrenv and getrenv().require) or require
        local ok, mod = pcall(req, syn)
        if ok and type(mod) == "table" then _syncMod = mod end
    end
    local getFn = type(_syncMod) == "table" and rawget(_syncMod, "Get")
    local gf = getfenv or (debug and debug.getfenv)
    if type(getFn) ~= "function" or type(gf) ~= "function" then return false end
    local ok, env = pcall(gf, getFn)
    if not ok or type(env) ~= "table" then return false end
    local ch = rawget(env, "Channels")
    if type(ch) ~= "table" then return false end
    _xchan = ch
    return true
end
local function _chans()
    if _xchan then return _xchan end
    _trySenv()
    return _xchan
end

_G.XenSyncAll = function() return _chans() end
_G.XenSyncGet = function(idx)
    local t = _chans()
    if not t or idx == nil then return nil end
    local ok, cd = pcall(rawget, t, idx)
    if ok and type(cd) == "table" then return cd end
    local ok2, cd2 = pcall(function() return t[idx] end)
    if ok2 and type(cd2) == "table" then return cd2 end
    return nil
end
_G.SXE_IsPlotChanKey = function(k)
    return type(k) == "string" and #k >= 32 and string.find(k, "-", 1, true) ~= nil
end

_G.sProp = function(ch, key)
    if type(ch) ~= "table" or key == nil then return nil end
    local ct = rawget(ch, "CacheTable")
    if type(ct) ~= "table" then
        local okC, c2 = pcall(function() return ch.CacheTable end)
        if okC and type(c2) == "table" then ct = c2 end
    end
    if type(ct) ~= "table" then return nil end
    local v = rawget(ct, key)
    if v ~= nil then return v end
    local okV, v2 = pcall(function() return ct[key] end)
    if okV then return v2 end
    return nil
end

end
end
do
    local Players    = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
    local ANGVELSSpeed = { CARPET = 450 }
    if Config and Config.TpSettings then
        if tonumber(Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed) then
            ANGVELSSpeed.CARPET = math.min(450, tonumber(Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed))
        end
    end
    local _routeVia = "ligne"
    do
        pcall(function()
            settings().Physics.AllowSleep = false
            settings().Physics.PhysicsEnvironmentalThrottle = Enum.EnviromentalPhysicsThrottle.Disabled
            settings().Physics.ThrottleAdjustTime = 0
        end)
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("NetworkClient"):SetOutgoingKBPSLimit(100000)
        end)
    end
    local function getPlotChannel(plotName)
        local channel
        pcall(function() channel = _G.XenSyncGet(plotName) end)
        return channel
    end
    local function channelGet(channel, key)
        return _G.sProp(channel, key)
    end
    local _myPlotYes = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local function isMyPlot(channel)
        if not channel then return false end
        if _myPlotYes[channel] then return true end
        local owner = channelGet(channel, "Owner")
        if not owner then return false end
        local result = false
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(owner) == "Instance" and owner:IsA("Player") then
                result = owner.UserId == LP.UserId
            elseif type(owner) == "table" and owner.UserId then
                result = owner.UserId == LP.UserId
            elseif typeof(owner) == "Instance" then
                result = owner == LP
            end
        end)
        if result then _myPlotYes[channel] = true end
        return result
    end
    _G.SXE_IsMyPlot = isMyPlot
    local function isPlotUnlocked(plotName)
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            local channel = getPlotChannel(plotName)
            if not channel then return false end
            return channelGet(channel, "BlockEndTimeFirstFloor") == nil
        end)
        return ok and (res == true)
    end
    local _podiumPos = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local _slotStr = {}
    local function _slotKey(slot)
        local s = _slotStr[slot]
        if s == nil then s = tostring(slot); _slotStr[slot] = s end
        return s
    end
    local function _cheapPos(inst)
        if not inst then return nil end
        if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst.Position end
        local pp = inst.PrimaryPart
        if pp then return pp.Position end
        local p = inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
        return p and p.Position or nil
    end
    local function _plotWorldPos(plot)
        local p = _cheapPos(plot)
        if p then return p end
        for _, n in ipairs({ "PlotSign", "MainRoot", "Base", "Floor", "Spawn" }) do
            p = _cheapPos(plot:FindFirstChild(n))
            if p then return p end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function getPetPosition(plot, slot)
        if not plot then return nil end
        slot = _slotKey(slot)
        local sub = _podiumPos[plot]
        if sub == nil then sub = {}; _podiumPos[plot] = sub end
        local hit = sub[slot]
        if hit then return hit end
        local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not podiums then return nil end
        local podium = podiums:FindFirstChild(slot)
        if not podium then
            for _, ch in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                if tostring(ch.Name) == slot then podium = ch; break end
            end
        end
        if not podium then return nil end
        local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
        local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
        local pos = _cheapPos(spawn) or _cheapPos(base) or _cheapPos(podium)
        if pos then
            sub[slot] = pos
            return pos
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function _findPlot(plotName)
        local Plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not Plots or plotName == nil then return nil end
        local name = tostring(plotName)
        local plot = Plots:FindFirstChild(name)
        if plot then return plot end
        for _, pl in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
            if pl.Name == name or tostring(pl.Name) == name then return pl end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function _ensurePetPos(p)
        if not p then return nil end
        if p.position then return p.position end
        local plot = _findPlot(p.plot)
        if not plot then return nil end
        local pos = getPetPosition(plot, p.slot)
        if pos then
            p.position = pos
            return pos
        end
        local hint = _plotWorldPos(plot)
        if hint then p.plotHint = hint end
        return nil
    end
    local _BLOCKING_MACHINE_TYPES = { Fuse=true, Duel=true, Trade=true, Crafting=true }
    local function _ANGVELSIsFusing(animalData)
        if type(animalData) ~= "table" then return false end
        local m = animalData.Machine
        if type(m) ~= "table" then return false end
        return _BLOCKING_MACHINE_TYPES[m.Type] == true and m.Active == true
    end
    local _prioRank = {}
    do
        local list = (Config and Config.PriorityList) or priorityList
        if type(list) == "table" then
            for i, n in ipairs(list) do
                if type(n) == "string" then _prioRank[string.lower(n)] = i end
            end
        end
    end
    local _MUT_RANK = {
        bloodrot = 1,
        crystal = 2,
        phantom = 3,
        candy = 4,
        lava = 5,
        yinyang = 6,
        yingyang = 6,
        radioactive = 7,
        galaxy = 9,
        divine = 10,
        divin = 10,
        rainbow = 11,
    }
    local function _adMut(ad)
        if type(ad) ~= "table" then return "" end
        local m = ad.Mutation or ad.mutation or ad.Mut
        if type(m) == "table" then
            m = m.Name or m.Index or m.Type or m[1]
        end
        if type(m) ~= "string" then return "" end
        return m
    end
    local function _mutRank(m)
        local k = tostring(m or ""):lower():gsub("[%s%-_]", "")
        return _MUT_RANK[k] or 99
    end
    local function _bestPrioOnBase(plotName, animalList)
        local best, bestRank, bestMut = nil, math.huge, 99
        if type(animalList) ~= "table" then return nil end
        for slot, animalData in pairs(animalList) do
            if type(animalData) ~= "table" then continue end
            local animalName = animalData.Index
            if not animalName then continue end
            local rank = _prioRank[string.lower(animalName)]
            if not rank then continue end
            if _ANGVELSIsFusing(animalData) then continue end
            local mut = _adMut(animalData)
            local mutRank = _mutRank(mut)
            local same = best and string.lower(animalName) == string.lower(best.index)
            local take = false
            if not best then
                take = true
            elseif rank < bestRank then
                take = true
            elseif same and mutRank < bestMut then
                take = true
            end
            if take then
                bestRank = rank
                bestMut = mutRank
                best = {
                    name = animalName, index = animalName, position = nil,
                    plotHint = nil, mutation = mut,
                    plot = tostring(plotName), slot = tostring(slot),
                }
            end
        end
        return best
    end
    local _prioSlots, _podHook, _myPodNuke = {}, {}, {}
    local function nukePlotPodiums(plot)
        if not (plot and plot.Parent) then return end
        if _myPodNuke[plot] then
            local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if pods then
                local kids = pods:GetChildren()
                for i = 1, #kids do _G.SXE_Kill(kids[i]) end
            end
            return
        end
        _myPodNuke[plot] = true
        local function wipe(folder)
            if not folder then return end
            local kids = folder:GetChildren()
            for i = 1, #kids do _G.SXE_Kill(kids[i]) end
            folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
                _G.SXE_Kill(ch)
            end)
        end
        wipe(plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums"))
        plot.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if ch and ch.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then wipe(ch) end
        end)
    end
    local function stripNonPrioPodiums(plot)
        if not (plot and plot.Parent) then return end
        if _G.__SXE_MyPlot and plot.Name == _G.__SXE_MyPlot then
            nukePlotPodiums(plot)
            return
        end
        local set = _G.SXE_PrioSet
        if type(set) ~= "table" then return end
        local ch
        pcall(function() ch = _G.XenSyncGet(plot.Name) end)
        if ch and isMyPlot(ch) then
            _G.__SXE_MyPlot = plot.Name
            nukePlotPodiums(plot)
            return
        end
        local list = ch and channelGet(ch, "AnimalList")
        if type(list) ~= "table" then return end
        local keepSlots = {}
        for slot, ad in pairs(list) do
            if type(ad) == "table" and type(ad.Index) == "string"
                and set[string.lower(ad.Index)] then
                keepSlots[tostring(slot)] = true
            end
        end
        if not next(keepSlots) then return end
        _prioSlots[plot.Name] = keepSlots
        local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if not pods then return end
        local kids = pods:GetChildren()
        for i = 1, #kids do
            local slotInst = kids[i]
            if not keepSlots[tostring(slotInst.Name)] then
                _G.SXE_Kill(slotInst)
            end
        end
        if not _podHook[plot] then
            _podHook[plot] = true
            pods.ChildAdded:Connect(function(slotInst)
                local ks = _prioSlots[plot.Name]
                if slotInst and ks and not ks[tostring(slotInst.Name)] then
                    _G.SXE_Kill(slotInst)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    _G.SXE_NukePlotPodiums = nukePlotPodiums
    _G.SXE_StripNonPrioPodiums = stripNonPrioPodiums
    -- Nombre de scans consecutifs "aucune info" avant destruction (0.5s/scan)
    local EMPTY_CONFIRM = 4
    local _emptyStreak = {}
    _G.__SXE_Protected = _G.__SXE_Protected or {}

    -- Tue un plot SEULEMENT si aucun brainrot prio n'y est visible cote client.
    -- C'est la garde finale universelle: rien ne peut la contourner.
    local function safeKillPlot(plot, set)
        if not plot or not plot.Parent then return end
        if plot.Name == _G.__SXE_MyPlot then return end
        if plot.Name == _G.__SXE_KeepPlot then return end
        if _G.__SXE_Protected[plot.Name] then return end
        for _, c in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("Model") and (c:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or c:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart"))
                and set[string.lower(c.Name)] then
                _G.__SXE_Protected[plot.Name] = true
                return
            end
        end
        local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if pods then
            for _, d in ipairs(pods:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("Model") and d.Name ~= "Claim" and set[string.lower(d.Name)] then
                    _G.__SXE_Protected[plot.Name] = true
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        _emptyStreak[plot.Name] = nil
        _G.SXE_Kill(plot)
    end

    local function stripNoPrioPlots()
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plots then return end
        if not _G.__SXE_MyPlot then return end
        if _G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
        local set = _G.SXE_PrioSet
        if type(set) ~= "table" then return end
        -- Le registre sync doit etre peuple
        local syncReady = false
        do
            local reg = _G.XenSyncAll and _G.XenSyncAll()
            if type(reg) == "table" then
                local isKey = _G.SXE_IsPlotChanKey
                for nm in next, reg do
                    if (not isKey) or isKey(nm) then syncReady = true break end
                end
            end
        end
        if not syncReady then return end

        local keep = _G.__SXE_KeepPlot
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local pname = plot.Name
            if pname == _G.__SXE_MyPlot then continue end

            local ch
            pcall(function() ch = _G.XenSyncGet(pname) end)
            if ch and isMyPlot(ch) then
                _G.__SXE_MyPlot = pname
                continue
            end

            -- Prio visible cote client -> proteger definitivement
            local clientPrio = false
            for _, c in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("Model") and (c:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or c:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart"))
                    and set[string.lower(c.Name)] then
                    clientPrio = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not clientPrio then
                local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                if pods then
                    for _, d in ipairs(pods:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("Model") and d.Name ~= "Claim" and set[string.lower(d.Name)] then
                            clientPrio = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if clientPrio then
                _G.__SXE_Protected[pname] = true
                _emptyStreak[pname] = nil
                pcall(stripNonPrioPodiums, plot)
                continue
            end

            -- Prio cote serveur (AnimalList charge)
            local list = ch and channelGet(ch, "AnimalList")
            if type(list) == "table" then
                local serverPrio = false
                for _, ad in pairs(list) do
                    if type(ad) == "table" and type(ad.Index) == "string"
                        and set[string.lower(ad.Index)] then
                        serverPrio = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                _emptyStreak[pname] = nil
                if serverPrio then
                    _G.__SXE_Protected[pname] = true
                    pcall(stripNonPrioPodiums, plot)
                else
                    -- Preuve positive: AnimalList charge et aucun prio
                    _G.__SXE_Protected[pname] = nil
                    if pname == keep then
                        pcall(stripNonPrioPodiums, plot)
                    else
                        safeKillPlot(plot, set)
                    end
                end
                continue
            end

            -- Plot protege ou cible sans info serveur -> on garde
            if pname == keep or _G.__SXE_Protected[pname] then
                pcall(stripNonPrioPodiums, plot)
                continue
            end

            -- Aucune info (pas d'AnimalList): ne detruire qu'apres EMPTY_CONFIRM scans
            -- consecutifs sans le moindre brainrot visible.
            local anyModel = false
            for _, c in ipairs(plot:GetChildren()) do
                if c:IsA("Model") and (c:FindFirstChild("RootPart") or c:FindFirstChild("FakeRootPart")) then
                    anyModel = true
                    break
                end
            end
            if not anyModel then
                local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
                if pods then
                    for _, d in ipairs(pods:GetDescendants()) do
                        if d:IsA("Model") and d.Name ~= "Claim" then
                            anyModel = true
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if anyModel then
                -- Des brainrots sont la mais l'info serveur manque: on attend, on ne detruit pas
                _emptyStreak[pname] = nil
                continue
            end
            local s = (_emptyStreak[pname] or 0) + 1
            _emptyStreak[pname] = s
            if s >= EMPTY_CONFIRM then
                safeKillPlot(plot, set)
            end
        end
    end
    local function scanAllPets()
        local pets = {}
        local isKey = _G.SXE_IsPlotChanKey
        local best, bestPlot
        local reg = _G.XenSyncAll and _G.XenSyncAll()
        if type(reg) == "table" then
            for name, ch in next, reg do
                if not (isKey and isKey(name)) then continue end
                if not ch then continue end
                if isMyPlot(ch) then
                    _G.__SXE_MyPlot = tostring(name)
                    continue
                end
                local cand = _bestPrioOnBase(name, channelGet(ch, "AnimalList"))
                if cand then
                    _G.__SXE_Protected[tostring(name)] = true
                    if not best then
                        best, bestPlot = cand, tostring(name)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if best then
            _G.__SXE_KeepPlot = tostring(bestPlot)
        end
        if _G.__SXE_StripDone and not _G.__SXE_VelFlying then
            pcall(stripNoPrioPlots)
        end
        if best then
            pets[1] = best
            pcall(_G.SXE_LockPrioPlot, bestPlot)
        end
        return pets
    end
    _G.SXE_ScanPets = scanAllPets
    -- Periodic scan: rapide au debut pour nettoyer vite, puis lent pour + de FPS
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(1)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while true do
            -- 0.4s pendant les 8 premieres secondes, puis 1.5s ensuite
            local fast = (os.clock() - t0) < 8
            task.wait(fast and 0.4 or 1.5)
            if _G.__SXE_StripDone and not _G.__SXE_VelFlying and not _G.__SXE_TPStarted then
                pcall(scanAllPets)
            end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < 20 do
            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if plots then
                for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
                    local ch
                    pcall(function() ch = _G.XenSyncGet(plot.Name) end)
                    if ch and isMyPlot(ch) then
                        _G.__SXE_MyPlot = plot.Name
                        pcall(nukePlotPodiums, plot)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
            task.wait()
        end
    end)
    local UPPER = {
        B = {{coord=Vector3.new(-487.921448,16.850713,-75.768013),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-332.379730,16.850722,-75.762100),facing="NORTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-487.134918,16.850713,-18.094154),facing="SOUTH"},{coord=Vector3.new(-316.300171,16.850713,-17.845898),facing="SOUTH"}},

    }
    local UPPER_Y_THRESHOLD = 7
    local TALL_PETS = { ["La Secret Combinasion"]=true, ["La Jolly Grande"]=true }
    local TALL_OFFSET = 3
    local BASES_LOW = {
        [1]=Vector3.new(-476.52,-2,220.94),[2]=Vector3.new(-476.52,-2,113.77),
        [3]=Vector3.new(-476.52,-2,6.18),[4]=Vector3.new(-476.52,-2,-101.07),

        [7]=Vector3.new(-339.48,18,6.25),[8]=Vector3.new(-339.48,18,-99.73),
    }
    -- Toujours l'emprise 3 etages (laser idx 1 west / idx 7 east). Etage/laser live ignorés.
    local _PLOT_BOXES = {
        [1] = { minX = -525.25, maxX = -479.75, minY = -10.10, maxY = 43.90, minZ = 196.61, maxZ = 242.11 },
        [2] = { minX = -525.25, maxX = -479.75, minY = -10.10, maxY = 43.90, minZ = 89.61, maxZ = 135.11 },
