    local function findAdorneeGlobal(entry)
        local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(entry.plot)
        local pods = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        local pod = pods and pods:FindFirstChild(tostring(entry.slot))
        local base = pod and pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
        return base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
    end
    local function adaptPet(pet)
        if not pet then return end
        pet.uid = tostring(pet.plot).."_"..tostring(pet.slot)
        pet.slot = tostring(pet.slot)
        local reg = _G.XenSyncAll and _G.XenSyncAll()
        local ch = type(reg) == "table" and reg[pet.plot]
        local owner = ch and channelGet(ch, "Owner")
        if typeof(owner) == "Instance" then pet.owner = owner.Name
        elseif type(owner) == "number" then
            local pl = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(owner); pet.owner = pl and pl.Name
        elseif type(owner) == "string" then pet.owner = owner
        elseif type(owner) == "table" then
            local player = tonumber(owner.UserId) and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(tonumber(owner.UserId))
            pet.owner = player and player.Name or owner.Name
        end
        return pet
    end
    local function targetAlive(entry)
        if type(entry) ~= "table" or not entry.plot or not entry.slot or entry.plot==_G.__SXE_MyPlot then return false end
        local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        local plot=plots and plots:FindFirstChild(entry.plot)
        local pods=plot and plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        local pod=pods and pods:FindFirstChild(tostring(entry.slot))
        if not pod then return false end
        local ch=_G.XenSyncGet and _G.XenSyncGet(entry.plot)
        if not ch or (_G.SXE_IsMyPlot and _G.SXE_IsMyPlot(ch)) or not channelGet(ch,"Owner") then return false end
        local animals=channelGet(ch,"AnimalList")
        local data=type(animals)=="table" and (animals[tonumber(entry.slot)] or animals[tostring(entry.slot)])
        if type(data)~="table" or _ANGVELSIsFusing(data) then return false end
        if data.Index~=(entry.index or entry.name) then return false end
        if entry.owner and not Players:FindFirstChild(entry.owner) then return false end
        return true
    end
    local targetRevision=0
    _G.SXE_StealTargetAPI={
        entries=function() return SharedState.AllAnimalsCache end,
        alive=targetAlive,
        selected=function() return _G.SXE_SelectedStealTarget end,
        select=function(entry)
            if not targetAlive(entry) then return false end
            targetRevision=targetRevision+1
            local revision=targetRevision
            _nearestOn=false
            nearestPaint(false)
            _G.SXE_SelectedStealTarget=entry
            armSteal(entry)
            manualTarget=entry
            pcall(_G.SXE_LockPrioPlot,entry.plot)
            -- Hub1 retargets on selection; serialize that with the existing Message40 TP.
            if not LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") and not LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("StealingIndex") then
                task.spawn(function()
                    local started=os.clock()
                    while _G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.__SXE_VelFlying or _G.RecoveryInProgress do
                        if revision~=targetRevision or os.clock()-started>35 then return end
                        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    end
                    if revision~=targetRevision or not targetAlive(entry) or LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then return end
                    if _G.SXE_DoVelocityTP then _G.SXE_DoVelocityTP(entry) end
                end)
            end
            return true
        end,
    }
    _G.JAF_M2 = function(ax,az,bx,bz) return math.sqrt((ax-bx)^2+(az-bz)^2) end
    _G.JAF_Ping = function()
        if _G.JAF_PingAdapt ~= true then return 0 end
        local ok,v = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() end)
        return ok and v or 0
    end
    _G.JAF_PrioScore = function(entry)
        local rank = _prioRank[string.lower(entry.name or "")]
        if not rank then return 999999 end
        return rank * 1000 + _mutRank(entry.mutation)
    end
    _G.JAF_IsBeingStolen = function(entry)
        local reg = _G.XenSyncAll and _G.XenSyncAll()
        local ch = type(reg) == "table" and reg[entry.plot]
        local animals = ch and channelGet(ch,"AnimalList")
        local data = type(animals) == "table" and (animals[tonumber(entry.slot)] or animals[entry.slot])
        local id = type(data) == "table" and data.Steal
        local pl = type(id) == "number" and Players:GetPlayerByUserId(id)
        return pl ~= nil and pl ~= LocalPlayer and pl:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
    end
    local refreshAt = 0
    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        stealNearest = _nearestOn == true
        local selected=_G.SXE_SelectedStealTarget
        if selected and not targetAlive(selected) then
            _G.SXE_SelectedStealTarget=nil
            if _stealTarget and _stealTarget.uid==selected.uid then _stealTarget=nil end
            selected=nil
        end
        manualTarget = not stealNearest and adaptPet(selected or _stealTarget) or nil
        _G.JAF_TpActive = (_G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.__SXE_VelFlying or _G.RecoveryInProgress) == true
        SharedState._currentTpTarget = adaptPet(_stealTarget)
        if os.clock() - refreshAt >= 0.1 then
            refreshAt = os.clock()
            local entries = {}
            local reg = _G.XenSyncAll and _G.XenSyncAll()
            if type(reg) == "table" then
                for plotName, ch in pairs(reg) do
                    if _G.SXE_IsPlotChanKey(plotName) and not (_G.SXE_IsMyPlot and _G.SXE_IsMyPlot(ch)) then
                        local animals = channelGet(ch, "AnimalList")
                        if type(animals) == "table" then
                            for slot, data in pairs(animals) do
                                if type(data) == "table" and type(data.Index) == "string"
                                    and _prioRank[string.lower(data.Index)] and not _ANGVELSIsFusing(data) then
                                    entries[#entries+1] = adaptPet({name=data.Index, index=data.Index, plot=tostring(plotName), slot=tostring(slot), mutation=_adMut(data)})
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            SharedState.AllAnimalsCache = entries
        end
    end)
local isAutoStealing = false
local StealData      = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local PromptCache    = {}
local PromptIdx      = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local PromptIdxDirty = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local PromptIdxConn  = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local _stealStart    = 0
local _stealProgress = 0
local _holdEpoch     = 0
local _ragUntil; _ragUntil = function() 
    return math.max(tonumber(LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")) or 0, tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollUntil) or 0)
end
local _ragBlockedNow; _ragBlockedNow = function() 
    return (_ragUntil() - workspace:GetServerTimeNow() + (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollLatePad) or 0.1)) > 0
        or (tick() - (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPhysLastT) or 0)) < (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPhysWindow) or 0.4)
end
local _podOf; _podOf = function(uid) 
    if not uid then return nil end
    local pn, sl = tostring(uid):match("^(.*)_([^_]+)$")
    if not (pn and sl) then return nil end
    local pl = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    pl = pl and pl:FindFirstChild(pn)
    pl = pl and pl:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
    return pl and pl:FindFirstChild(sl)
end
local _grabCB; _grabCB = function(p) 
    local d = { hold = {}, trigger = {} }
    local ok, cs = pcall(getconnections, p.PromptButtonHoldBegan)
    if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
        for _, c in ipairs(cs) do if type(c.Function) == "function" then d.hold[#d.hold + 1] = c.Function end end
    end
    ok, cs = pcall(getconnections, p.Triggered)
    if ok and type(cs) == "table" then
        for _, c in ipairs(cs) do if type(c.Function) == "function" then d.trigger[#d.trigger + 1] = c.Function end end
    end
    return d
end
local _fireTrigger; _fireTrigger = function(p, d) 
    if fireproximityprompt and _G.JAF_StealUseFPP ~= false then pcall(fireproximityprompt, p); return end
    if d then for _, f in ipairs(d.trigger) do task.spawn(f) end
        if #d.trigger == 0 and fireproximityprompt then pcall(fireproximityprompt, p) end end
end
local _apCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local _apFromHold; _apFromHold = function(p, d) 
    local ap = _apCache[p]
    if ap ~= nil then return ap or nil end
    if debug and debug.getupvalues and d then
        for _, f in ipairs(d.hold) do
            local ok, ups = pcall(debug.getupvalues, f)
            if ok then for _, v in pairs(ups) do
                if type(v) == "table" and rawget(v, "State") ~= nil and rawget(v, "ProximityPrompt") ~= nil then
                    _apCache[p] = v
                    return v
                end
            end end
        end
    end
    _apCache[p] = false
    return nil
end
local _fireHoldForced; _fireHoldForced = function(p, d) 
    if not d or #d.hold == 0 then return false end
    local ap = _apFromHold(p, d)
    if ap and ap.State ~= "Steal" then
        local _os = ap.State
        ap.State = "Steal"
        for _, f in ipairs(d.hold) do pcall(f) end
        ap.State = _os
        return true
    end
    if not ap and p and p.Enabled == false then return false end
    for _, f in ipairs(d.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
    return true
end
-- Proven one-shot steal on a single prompt, using the SAME primitive the normal
-- autosteal uses (hold-began callbacks with the anti-cheat State forced to "Steal",
-- then the trigger). fireproximityprompt alone does NOT complete a steal here.
_G.JAF_ForceSteal = function(p) 
    if not (p and p.Parent and p.Enabled) then return false end
    local d = _grabCB(p)
    _fireHoldForced(p, d)
    _fireTrigger(p, d)
    return true
end
local isMyPlot; isMyPlot = function(plotName) 
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    local plot  = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
    local sign  = plot and plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    local yb    = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
    return plotName == _G.__SXE_MyPlot or (yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") and yb.Enabled == true) or false
end
local function findPrompt(a) 
    if not a then return nil end
    if a.isWalking then if a.prompt and a.prompt.Parent and a.prompt.Enabled then return a.prompt end return nil end
    local cp = PromptCache[a.uid]
    if cp and cp.Parent and cp.Enabled and cp.ActionText == "Steal" then return cp end
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots"); if not plots then return nil end
    local plot  = plots:FindFirstChild(a.plot);     if not plot  then return nil end
    local pods  = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums"); if not pods then return nil end
    local pod   = pods:FindFirstChild(a.slot);      if not pod   then return nil end
    local base  = pod:FindFirstChild("Base");        if not base  then return nil end
    local sp    = base:FindFirstChild("Spawn");      if not sp    then return nil end
    local att   = sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
    if att then
        for _, p in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
            if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and p.Enabled and p.ActionText == "Steal" then PromptCache[a.uid] = p; return p end
        end end
    local startPos = sp.Position
    local best, bd = nil, math.huge
    local _pl = PromptIdx[plot]
    if not _pl or PromptIdxDirty[plot] then
        _pl = {}
        for _, d in ipairs(plot:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then _pl[#_pl + 1] = d end end
        PromptIdx[plot], PromptIdxDirty[plot] = _pl, nil
        if not PromptIdxConn[plot] then
            local function _mark(d) 
if d:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then PromptIdxDirty[plot] = true end end
            PromptIdxConn[plot] = { plot.DescendantAdded:Connect(_mark), plot.DescendantRemoving:Connect(_mark), }
        end end
    for _, d in ipairs(_pl) do
        if d.Parent and d.Enabled and d.ActionText == "Steal" then
            local part = d.Parent
            local pp
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then pp = part.Position
            elseif part:IsA("Attachment") and part.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then pp = part.Parent.Position end
            if pp then
                local dx, dz = pp.X - startPos.X, pp.Z - startPos.Z
                if (dx * dx + dz * dz) < 25 and pp.Y > startPos.Y then
                    local yd = pp.Y - startPos.Y
                    if yd < bd then bd = yd; best = d end
                end end end end
    if best then PromptCache[a.uid] = best end
    return best
end
local _ownerPresent = {}
do
    for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do _ownerPresent[pl.Name] = true end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(pl) 
_ownerPresent[pl.Name] = true end)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(pl) 
_ownerPresent[pl.Name] = nil end)
end
local _stealable; _stealable = function(a, me) 
    if not a or a.owner == me or a.plot == _G.__SXE_MyPlot then return false end
    local livePlots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not (livePlots and a.plot and livePlots:FindFirstChild(a.plot)) then return false end
    if _G.JAF_SkipLeftOwners == false then return true end
    return a.owner == nil or _ownerPresent[a.owner] == true
end
local resolvePriorityTarget; resolvePriorityTarget = function() 
    local _lpName = LocalPlayer.Name
    local _all = SharedState.AllAnimalsCache
    if stealNearest then
        local _, hrp = getChar()
        local bestEntry = nil
        if hrp then
            local bestDist = math.huge
            local _hrpPos = hrp.Position
            for _, a in ipairs(_all) do
                if _stealable(a, _lpName) then
                    local part = findAdorneeGlobal(a)
                    if part then local d = (_hrpPos - part.Position).Magnitude if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; bestEntry = a end end
                end end
        end
        return bestEntry
    end
    if manualTarget then return manualTarget end
    if (Config.UsePriority ~= false) then
        local best, bestScore = nil, math.huge
        local _prio = _G.JAF_PrioScore
        for _, a in ipairs(_all) do
            if a and a.name and _stealable(a, _lpName) then local s = _prio(a) if s < bestScore then bestScore, best = s, a end end
        end
        if best and bestScore < 999999 then return best end
    end
    for _, a in ipairs(_all) do if _stealable(a, _lpName) then return a end end
    return nil
end
_G.JAF_ResolveBest = resolvePriorityTarget
local findNearestPrompt; findNearestPrompt = function(priorityTarget) 
    local _, hrp = getChar()
    if not hrp then return end
    local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end
    priorityTarget = priorityTarget or resolvePriorityTarget()
    if not priorityTarget then return end
    local _hrpPos = hrp.Position
    local _radius = Config.StealRadius or 95
    if priorityTarget.isWalking then
        local pr = priorityTarget.prompt
        if pr and pr.Parent and pr.Enabled then
            local model = priorityTarget.model
            local part = model and model.Parent and model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
            if part then local pp = part.Position if _G.JAF_M2(pp.X, pp.Z, _hrpPos.X, _hrpPos.Z) <= _radius then return pr end end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(priorityTarget.plot)
    if not plot or isMyPlot(plot.Name) then return nil end
    local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
    local pod  = pods and pods:FindFirstChild(priorityTarget.slot)
    local base = pod and pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
    local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
    if not spawn then return nil end
    local sp = spawn.Position
    if _G.JAF_M2(sp.X, sp.Z, _hrpPos.X, _hrpPos.Z) > _radius then return nil end
    local att = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
    if not att then return nil end
    local nearest
    for _, p in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and p.Enabled and p.ActionText:find("Steal") then nearest = p end end
    return nearest
end
local StealCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local promptDistXZ; promptDistXZ = function(prompt) 
    local _, hrp = getChar()
    if not hrp then return nil end
    local par = prompt and prompt.Parent
    local pos
    if par then
        if par:IsA("Attachment") then pos = par.WorldPosition
        elseif par:IsA("BasePart") then pos = par.Position
        else
            local bp = par:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("BasePart")
            if bp then pos = bp.Position end
        end end
    if not pos then return nil end
    local hp = hrp.Position
    return _G.JAF_M2(pos.X, pos.Z, hp.X, hp.Z), (pos - hp).Magnitude
end
local _pivotPos = function(m) 
return m:GetPivot().Position end
local function executeSteal(prompt, heldUid, preHold) 
    if not SAB_LIVE() then return end
    if isAutoStealing then return end
    local function _tpBanking() 
        return preHold == true and _G.JAF_StealPreHold ~= false and _G.JAF_TpActive == true and heldUid ~= nil
           and SharedState._currentTpTarget ~= nil and SharedState._currentTpTarget.uid == heldUid
    end
    local _d = promptDistXZ(prompt)
    if _d and _d > (Config.StealRadius or 95) and not _tpBanking() then return end
    local data = StealData[prompt]
    if not data then data = _grabCB(prompt); data.ready = true if #data.hold > 0 and (#data.trigger > 0 or _G.JAF_StealUseFPP ~= false) then StealData[prompt] = data end end
    if not data.ready then return end
    data.ready = false
    isAutoStealing = true
    _stealStart = tick()
    task.spawn(function() 
        local oldDuration = prompt.HoldDuration
        prompt.HoldDuration = 0
        local _beginT, _inherited = 0, false
        local _hAge = _holdEpoch > 0 and (tick() - _holdEpoch) or math.huge
        if _G.JAF_StealHoldCarry ~= false and prompt.Enabled and _hAge <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCarryMax) or 1.25) then
            _beginT, _inherited = _holdEpoch, true
        else
            for _, f in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
            _beginT = prompt.Enabled and tick() or 0
            if _beginT > 0 and #data.hold > 0 then _holdEpoch = _beginT end
        end
        local _beganOk, _recapN, _instaCommitted = (_inherited or #data.hold > 0), 0, false
        local _dur = Config.StealDuration
        if not _dur then
            local _srv = (_G.JAF_StealUsePromptHold == true) and tonumber(oldDuration) or nil
            _dur = ((_srv and _srv > 0.1 and _srv) or 1.3) + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealHoldPad) or 0)
        end
        _G.JAF_StealDurNow = _dur
        local _maxHold = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealMaxHold) or (_dur + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealExtendWindow) or 1.3))
        _G.JAF_StealCycleDur = _dur
        local _t0 = _inherited and _beginT or tick(); local _holdAbsStart = tick()
        if _inherited then _stealStart = _beginT end
        local _ragRet0 = _ragUntil()
        local _postGrace = (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPostGrace) or 0) + ((_ragRet0 > 0) and (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPostGrace) or 0) or 0)
        local _abort, _grabbed, _recycle, _didEnter = false, false, false, false
        local _clearFireUntil, _lastCommitT, _commitN = 0, 0, 0
        local _eligSince, _commitGraceUntil, _tpHold = 0, 0, false
        local _tpWasBlk, _wasDis, _disAt = false, false, nil
        local _carryChkT, _carryFlag, _carryLastT, _bankT, _campChkT = 0, false, 0, 0, 0
        local _promptGone, _goneAt, _goneConn = false, nil, nil
        local activePrompt, activeData = prompt, data
        local _podiumForDist = _podOf(heldUid)
        local function _petDistNow() 
            local _, _h = getChar()
            if not _h then return nil end
            if _podiumForDist and _podiumForDist.Parent then
                local ok, piv = pcall(_pivotPos, _podiumForDist)
                if ok and piv then return (piv - _h.Position).Magnitude end
            end
            local _, d3 = promptDistXZ(activePrompt); return d3
        end
        if _G.JAF_StealReacquire ~= false and heldUid and _podiumForDist then
            _goneConn = _podiumForDist.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc) 
                if _promptGone and desc:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and tostring(desc.ActionText or ""):find("Steal") then
                    activePrompt, activeData = desc, _grabCB(desc)
                    pcall(function() 
desc.HoldDuration = 0 end)
                    for _, f in ipairs(activeData.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
                    _beginT = desc.Enabled and tick() or 0
                    if _beginT > 0 and #activeData.hold > 0 then _holdEpoch = _beginT end
                    _beganOk = #activeData.hold > 0
                    _wasDis, _disAt, _bankT = false, nil, 0
                    _G.JAF_StealCarryWait = nil
                    _promptGone, _goneAt = false, nil
                    _stealStart = tick()
                    _t0, _commitN, _lastCommitT, _recycle = tick(), 0, 0, false
                end end) end
        repeat
            if manualTarget ~= nil and heldUid ~= nil and manualTarget.uid ~= heldUid then _abort = true; break end
            if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then _grabbed = true; break end
            if _goneConn and not activePrompt.Parent and not _promptGone then _promptGone = true; _goneAt = tick() end
            local _bestSwitch = manualTarget == nil and heldUid ~= nil and _G.JAF_StealBestSwitch ~= false and _G.JAF_ResolveBest
            if _promptGone then
                _G.JAF_StealInCommitRange = false
                if _bankT == 0 or (tick() - _bankT) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealBankRefresh) or 2.5) then
                    if _fireHoldForced(activePrompt, activeData) then _bankT = tick() _holdEpoch = _bankT end
                end
                _G.JAF_StealCarryWait = tick() - _goneAt
                _G.JAF_StealCarryT0 = _bankT
                if (tick() - _goneAt) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPromptGoneWait) or 6.5) then break end
                if _bestSwitch then local _lb = _G.JAF_ResolveBest(); if _lb and _lb.uid and _lb.uid ~= heldUid then _abort = true; break end end
                _t0 = _t0 + (_G.JAF_StealFireInterval or 0.12)
                task.wait(_G.JAF_StealFireInterval or 0.12)
                continue
            end
            if activePrompt.Parent and not activePrompt.Enabled then
                if not _disAt then _disAt = tick() end
                _G.JAF_StealInCommitRange = false
                if (tick() - _carryChkT) > 0.25 then
                    _carryChkT = tick()
                    if #activeData.hold == 0 then activeData = _grabCB(activePrompt) end
                    local _cnow = false
                    if _G.JAF_IsBeingStolen then
                        for _, _sa in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) do if _sa and _sa.uid == heldUid then
                            local _cok, _cv = pcall(_G.JAF_IsBeingStolen, _sa)
                            _cnow = _cok and _cv == true
                            break
                        end end
                    end
                    if _cnow then _carryLastT = tick() end
                    _carryFlag = _cnow or (tick() - _carryLastT) < 1.0
                end
                if _bankT > 0 and (tick() - _bankT) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealBankRefresh) or 2.5) then _bankT = 0 end
                if _bankT == 0 and _G.JAF_StealPreBank ~= false then
                    if _fireHoldForced(activePrompt, activeData) then _bankT = tick() _holdEpoch = _bankT end
                end
                if (tick() - _campChkT) > 0.25 then
                    _campChkT = tick()
                    if _bankT == 0 and _G.JAF_StealPreBank ~= false then
                        for _, _sa in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) do
                            if _sa and _sa.uid ~= heldUid and _stealable(_sa, LocalPlayer.Name) then
                                local _bp = findPrompt(_sa)
                                if _bp and _bp.Enabled and _bp:GetAttribute("State") == "Steal" then
                                    local _bd = _grabCB(_bp)
                                    if #_bd.hold > 0 then for _, f in ipairs(_bd.hold) do task.spawn(f) end _bankT = tick() _holdEpoch = _bankT break end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if _bestSwitch then local _lb = _G.JAF_ResolveBest() if _lb and _lb.uid and _lb.uid ~= heldUid then
                        local _keep = false
                        for _, _sa in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) do if _sa and _sa.uid == heldUid then _keep = _stealable(_sa, LocalPlayer.Name) ~= false; break end end
                        if not _keep then _abort = true end
                    end end
                    if _abort then break end
                end
                if (not _carryFlag) and (tick() - math.max(_disAt, _carryLastT)) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealDisabledGrace) or 3) then break end
                _wasDis = true
                _G.JAF_StealCarryWait = tick() - _disAt
                _G.JAF_StealCarryT0 = _bankT
                local _dt = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPoll) or 0.03
                _stealStart = _bankT > 0 and _bankT or tick()
                _t0, _commitN, _lastCommitT, _recycle = tick(), 0, 0, false
                _G.JAF_StealExtending = false
                _G.JAF_StealCycleLeft = _maxHold
                _holdAbsStart = _holdAbsStart + (task.wait(_dt) or _dt)
                continue
            end
            if _wasDis then
                _wasDis, _disAt = false, nil
                _G.JAF_StealCarryWait = nil
                activeData = _grabCB(activePrompt)
                local _bAge = _bankT > 0 and (tick() - _bankT) or math.huge
                if _bAge <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealBankUseMax) or 2.3) then
                    _beginT = _bankT
                    _t0 = _bankT
                    _stealStart = _bankT
                    _beganOk = true
                else
                    for _, f in ipairs(activeData.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
                    _beginT = tick()
                    _beganOk = #activeData.hold > 0
                    if _beganOk then _holdEpoch = _beginT end
                    _t0 = tick()
                    _stealStart = tick()
                end
                _bankT = 0
                _commitN, _lastCommitT, _recycle = 0, 0, false
                _eligSince = tick() - (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealEntryDelay) or 0.3)
                _clearFireUntil = math.max(_clearFireUntil, tick() + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealDropSettle) or 0.5))
            end
            if not _beganOk then _stealStart = tick() end
            local _needTrig = _G.JAF_StealUseFPP == false
            if (#activeData.hold == 0 or (_needTrig and #activeData.trigger == 0)) and _recapN < (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealRecapMax) or 40) then
                _recapN = _recapN + 1
                local _rd = _grabCB(activePrompt)
                if #_rd.hold > 0 and (not _needTrig or #_rd.trigger > 0) then
                    activeData = _rd
                    if not _beganOk then
                        for _, f in ipairs(_rd.hold) do task.spawn(f) end
                        if activePrompt.Enabled then _beginT = tick() _holdEpoch = _beginT end
                        _beganOk = true
                        _stealStart = tick()
                        _t0, _commitN, _lastCommitT, _recycle = tick(), 0, 0, false
                    end end end
            local _hrDist, _hr3D = promptDistXZ(activePrompt)
            local _preBank = _tpBanking()
            if _hrDist and _hrDist > (Config.StealRadius or 95) and not _preBank then _abort = true; break end
            if _bestSwitch then local _lb = _G.JAF_ResolveBest() if _lb and _lb.uid and _lb.uid ~= heldUid then
                local _pd0 = _petDistNow()
                if _beginT == 0 or (tick() - _beginT) < (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHold) or _dur) or (_pd0 and _pd0 > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSwitchNear) or 25)) then _abort = true; break end
                local _keep = false
                for _, _sa in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) do if _sa and _sa.uid == heldUid then _keep = _stealable(_sa, LocalPlayer.Name) ~= false; break end end
                if not _keep then _abort = true; break end
            end end
            local _ragSync = _G.JAF_RagdollStealSync ~= false
            local _now = tick()
            if _ragSync then
                local _nr = _ragUntil(); local _remNow = _nr - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()
                if _nr > _ragRet0 + 0.3 and _remNow > 0.1 then
                    _ragRet0 = _nr
                    _t0 = math.max(_t0, _now + _remNow - _dur + (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollLatePad) or 0.1))
                    _stealStart = math.max(_stealStart, _t0)
                    _postGrace = (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPostGrace) or 0) + (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPostGrace) or 0)
                end end
            local _holdDone = (_now - _t0) >= (_dur + _postGrace)
            local _ragBlocked = _ragSync and _ragBlockedNow()
            if _ragSync and _G.JAF_RagdollHoldUntilClear ~= false and _holdDone and _ragBlocked then
                _clearFireUntil = _now + (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollClearWindow) or 0.35)
            end
            if _holdDone and not _grabbed and _G.JAF_StealRangeHold ~= false then _G.JAF_StealExtending = true end
            if _commitN == 0 and not _ragBlocked and (_beginT == 0 or (_now - _beginT) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHoldMax) or 2.5)) and activePrompt.Parent and activePrompt.Enabled then
                local _rhOk = _fireHoldForced(activePrompt, activeData)
                _beginT = tick()
                _holdEpoch = _rhOk and _beginT or 0
                _beganOk = #activeData.hold > 0
                _stealStart = tick()
                _t0 = math.max(_t0, tick())
            end
            local _petDist = _petDistNow()
            local _petInRange = _petDist and _petDist <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitRange) or 10)
            local _tpBlocking = (_G.JAF_StealTpGate ~= false) and (_G.JAF_TpActive == true) and SharedState._currentTpTarget ~= nil and SharedState._currentTpTarget.uid == heldUid
            if _tpWasBlk and not _tpBlocking and _G.JAF_StealTpRearm ~= false then
                local _relBar = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealTpRelBar) or 0
                if _relBar > 0 then
                    _t0 = math.max(_t0, _now - (_dur + _postGrace) + _relBar)
                    _stealStart = math.max(_stealStart, _t0)
                end
                _clearFireUntil = math.max(_clearFireUntil, _now + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealTpRelGrace) or 0.75))
            end
            _tpWasBlk = _tpBlocking
            if _petInRange and not _tpBlocking then if _eligSince == 0 then _eligSince = _now end else _eligSince = 0 end
            local _snapT = tonumber(_G.JAF_BrainrotSnapT) or 0
            local _instaFresh = _snapT > 0 and (_now - _snapT) < (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealInstaWindow) or 5)
            local _settleAnchor = _eligSince
            if _instaFresh and _snapT > _settleAnchor then _settleAnchor = _snapT end
            local _instaDelay = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealEntryDelayInsta)
            if not _instaDelay then
                _instaDelay = tonumber(_G.JAF_InstaSettle) or (tonumber(_G.JAF_InstaSettleStart) or 0.35)
                _G.JAF_InstaSettle = _instaDelay
            end
            local _effDelay = _instaFresh and _instaDelay or (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealEntryDelay) or 0.3)
            local _settled = (_eligSince > 0) and ((_now - _settleAnchor) >= _effDelay)
            local _inCommit = _holdDone and not _grabbed and not _ragBlocked and _petInRange and not _tpBlocking and _settled
                              and _beginT > 0 and (_now - _beginT) >= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHold) or _dur)
                              and (_now - _beginT) <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHoldMax) or 2.5)
            _tpHold = (_G.JAF_StealTpHold ~= false) and _tpBlocking and not _grabbed and _commitN == 0 and _holdDone
                      and (_now - _holdAbsStart) < (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealTpHoldMax) or 8)
            if _inCommit and _commitN == 0 then
                _lastCommitT, _commitN = _now, 1
                if _instaFresh then _instaCommitted = true end
                _fireTrigger(activePrompt, activeData)
            end
            if _inCommit and not _didEnter then
                _didEnter = true
                if _G.JAF_StealCommitSettle == true and ((not _preBank) or (_hr3D and _hr3D <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSettleNear) or 6))) then
                    local _, _shrp = getChar()
                    local _v = _shrp and _shrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local _minSpd = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitSettleSpeed) or 30
                    if _v and (_v.X * _v.X + _v.Z * _v.Z) > (_minSpd * _minSpd) then
                        pcall(function() 
_shrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, _v.Y, 0) end)
                    end end end
            if _commitN > 0 and not _grabbed and (_now - _lastCommitT) >= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitWait) or 0.4) then _recycle = true end
            _commitGraceUntil = 0
            if _G.JAF_StealHoldForSettle ~= false and not _grabbed and (_eligSince > 0 or _ragBlocked) then
                local _fm = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitFireMargin) or 0.06
                local _due = 0
                if _commitN == 0 then
                    if _eligSince > 0 and not _settled then _due = _settleAnchor + _effDelay + _fm end
                    if _beginT > 0 then
                        local _srvDue = _beginT + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHold) or _dur) + _fm
                        if _srvDue > _due then _due = _srvDue end
                    end
                    local _hdDue = _t0 + _dur + _postGrace + _fm
                    if _hdDue > _due then _due = _hdDue end
                else _due = _lastCommitT + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitWait) or 0.4) end
                if _due > 0 then _commitGraceUntil = _due end
            end
            _G.JAF_StealInCommitRange = _inCommit
            task.wait(tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPoll) or 0.03)
            if not _grabbed and (tick() - _t0) >= (_dur + _postGrace) then _G.JAF_StealExtending = true end
            local _base = _maxHold - (tick() - _t0)
            if _base > 0 then
                _G.JAF_StealCycleLeft = _base
            else
                local _ext = _commitGraceUntil - tick()
                if _tpHold then _ext = math.max(_ext, (_holdAbsStart + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealTpHoldMax) or 8)) - tick()) end
                _G.JAF_StealCycleLeft = math.max(_ext, 0)
            end
        until (not _promptGone and not _tpHold and not _disAt and ((tick() - _t0) >= _maxHold or _recycle) and tick() >= _clearFireUntil and tick() >= _commitGraceUntil)
              or (not _goneConn and not activePrompt.Parent)
              or (tick() - _holdAbsStart > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealHoldMaxTotal) or 20))
        _G.JAF_StealExtending = false
        _G.JAF_StealInCommitRange = false
        if _commitN > 0 or not _abort then _holdEpoch = 0 end
        do
            _G.JAF_StealCarryWait = nil
            local _why = _grabbed and "grab" or (_abort and "abort") or ((tick() - _holdAbsStart) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealHoldMaxTotal) or 20) and "cap") or (_disAt and "carry") or (_promptGone and "gone") or (_commitN > 0 and "fired") or "expire"
            local _pd = _petDistNow()
            local _r = _G.JAF_StealWhy if type(_r) ~= "table" then _r = {} _G.JAF_StealWhy = _r end
            _r[#_r + 1] = string.format("%s c=%d pet=%.1f tp=%s en=%s dur=%.2f age=%.2f", _why, _commitN, _pd or -1, tostring(_G.JAF_TpActive), tostring(activePrompt.Parent ~= nil and activePrompt.Enabled), _dur, tick() - _holdAbsStart)
            if #_r > 10 then table.remove(_r, 1) end
        end
        if _instaCommitted and tonumber(_G.JAF_StealEntryDelayInsta) == nil then
            local _cur = tonumber(_G.JAF_InstaSettle) or 0.35
            if _grabbed then
                _G.JAF_InstaSettle = math.max(_cur - (tonumber(_G.JAF_InstaSettleDecay) or 0.02), tonumber(_G.JAF_InstaSettleStart) or 0.35)
            else
                _G.JAF_InstaSettle = math.min(_cur + (tonumber(_G.JAF_InstaSettleStep) or 0.1), tonumber(_G.JAF_InstaSettleMax) or 0.45)
            end end
        if _goneConn then pcall(function() 
_goneConn:Disconnect() end) end
        if activePrompt.Parent and not _abort and not _grabbed and _commitN == 0 then
            local _fd = _petDistNow()
            if _fd and _fd <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCommitRange) or 10) and activePrompt.Enabled and _beginT > 0 and (tick() - _beginT) >= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHold) or _dur) and (tick() - _beginT) <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealSrvHoldMax) or 2.5) then _fireTrigger(activePrompt, activeData) end
        end
        if activePrompt.Parent then activePrompt.HoldDuration = oldDuration end
        data.ready = true
        isAutoStealing = false
    end) end
local function executeNearest(prompt, uid)
    if isAutoStealing or not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then return end
    local d = _grabCB(prompt)
    if #d.hold == 0 then return end
    isAutoStealing = true
    _stealStart = tick()
    _G.JAF_StealCycleDur = 1.2
    task.spawn(function()
        for _,fn in ipairs(d.hold) do task.spawn(fn) end
        task.wait(1.2)
        if _nearestOn and prompt.Parent and not LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
            for _,fn in ipairs(d.trigger) do task.spawn(fn) end
            if #d.trigger == 0 and fireproximityprompt then pcall(fireproximityprompt,prompt) end
        end
        isAutoStealing = false
    end)
end
local _asAcc, _asResolveDt = 1, 0.03
local _asP, _asFp, _asUid = nil, nil, nil
local _asPre = false
local _pgKey, _pgEntryT = nil, 0
local _pgPingEma, _pgPingJit, _pgPingT = nil, 0, 0
local stealPingSample; stealPingSample = function() 
    local now = tick()
    if now - _pgPingT < (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPingSampleDt) or 0.25) then return end
    _pgPingT = now
    local p = _G.JAF_Ping()
    if type(p) ~= "number" or p <= 0 then return end
    if not _pgPingEma then _pgPingEma, _pgPingJit = p, 0
    else
        local a = tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPingEmaAlpha) or 0.25
        _pgPingJit = _pgPingJit + (math.abs(p - _pgPingEma) - _pgPingJit) * a
        _pgPingEma = _pgPingEma + (p - _pgPingEma) * a
    end end
local stealPingWindow; stealPingWindow = function() 
    local rtt = _pgPingEma
    if not rtt then rtt = _G.JAF_Ping() end
    local ms = (rtt + (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPingJitterMult) or 1) * (_pgPingJit or 0)) * 1000
    ms = math.min(ms, tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPingDelayCapMs) or 1000)
    if ms < (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPingDelayMinMs) or 0) then ms = 0 end
    return ms / 1000
end
local stealPingGateReady; stealPingGateReady = function(prompt, uid) 
    if _G.JAF_StealPingDelay ~= true then return true end
    if not prompt then _pgKey = nil; return false end
    stealPingSample()
    local _d = promptDistXZ(prompt)
    if _d == nil then return true end
    if _d > (Config.StealRadius or 95) then _pgKey = nil; return false end
    local key = uid or prompt
    if _pgKey ~= key then _pgKey = key _pgEntryT = tick() end
    return tick() >= _pgEntryT + stealPingWindow()
end
_G.JAF_StealPingGateRearm = function() 
if _pgKey ~= nil then _pgEntryT = tick() end end
local _asEdgePrompt, _asEdgeConn = nil, nil
local function enableEdgeBurst(prompt) 
    if _G.ROSE_K7_OWNS_STEAL == true then return end
    if _G.JAF_EnableEdgeBurst ~= true then return end
    if not SAB_LIVE() then return end
    if not prompt or not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Enabled then return end
    if not (Config.AutoSteal ~= false) and not (_G.RoseCamp and _G.RoseCamp.active) then return end
    if _G.JAF_StealCarryGate ~= false and LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then return end
    if isAutoStealing then return end
    local _ebDist = promptDistXZ(prompt)
    if _ebDist and _ebDist > (Config.StealRadius or 95) then return end
    if not stealPingGateReady(prompt, _asUid) then return end
    local d = StealCache[prompt]
    if not d then d = _grabCB(prompt) if #d.hold == 0 and #d.trigger == 0 then return end StealCache[prompt] = d end
    local od = prompt.HoldDuration; prompt.HoldDuration = 0
    for _, fn in ipairs(d.hold) do pcall(fn) end
    for _ = 1, (tonumber(_G.JAF_EnableEdgeBurstCount) or 8) do
        for _, fn in ipairs(d.trigger) do pcall(fn) end
        if fireproximityprompt and #d.trigger == 0 then pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt) end
    end
    if prompt.Parent then prompt.HoldDuration = od end
end
_G.JAF_EdgeBurst = enableEdgeBurst   -- instant one-shot steal burst on a single prompt
local armEnableEdge; armEnableEdge = function(prompt) 
    if _G.JAF_EnableEdgeBurst ~= true then prompt = nil end
    if prompt == _asEdgePrompt then return end
    if _asEdgeConn then pcall(function() 
_asEdgeConn:Disconnect() end); _asEdgeConn = nil end
    _asEdgePrompt = prompt
    if not prompt then return end
    _asEdgeConn = prompt:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function() 
        if prompt == _asEdgePrompt and prompt.Enabled then enableEdgeBurst(prompt) end
    end) end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt) 
    if _G.ROSE_K7_OWNS_STEAL == true then return end
    if _G.RoseCamp and _G.RoseCamp.active then
        -- While camping, only steal the nearest brainrot in the camped base (never a
        -- global priority pick in a neighbouring base). No camp target = don't steal.
        if _G.RoseCamp._mt == nil then return end
    elseif not (Config.AutoSteal ~= false) then
        return
    end
    if isAutoStealing then return end
    if _G.JAF_StealCarryGate ~= false and LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then return end
    if _G.JAF_StealPingDelay ~= false then stealPingSample() end
    _asAcc = _asAcc + dt
    if _asAcc >= _asResolveDt or (_asP and not _asP.Parent) or (_asFp and not _asFp.Parent) then
        _asAcc = 0
        local tgt = resolvePriorityTarget()
        _asP = tgt and findNearestPrompt(tgt) or nil
        _asPre = false
        if not _asP and _G.JAF_StealPreHold ~= false and _G.JAF_TpActive == true and tgt and tgt.uid then
            local _ct = SharedState._currentTpTarget
            if _ct and _ct.uid == tgt.uid then
                local _cand = findPrompt(tgt)
                if _cand then
                    local _, _d3 = promptDistXZ(_cand)
                    if not _d3 or _d3 <= (tonumber(_G.JAF_StealPreHoldDist) or 95) then _asP, _asPre = _cand, true end
                end end end
        if not _asP and tgt and tgt.uid and _G.JAF_CampCarried ~= false and _G.JAF_IsBeingStolen then
            local _cok, _cv = pcall(_G.JAF_IsBeingStolen, tgt)
            if _cok and _cv == true then
                local _pod = _podOf(tgt.uid)
                local _sp = _pod and _pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
                _sp = _sp and _sp:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                local _att = _sp and _sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                if _att then for _, _pp in ipairs(_att:GetChildren()) do
                    if _pp:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and tostring(_pp.ActionText or ""):find("Steal") then
                        local _cd = promptDistXZ(_pp)
                        if _cd and _cd <= (Config.StealRadius or 95) then _asP = _pp end
                        break
                    end
                end end
            end
        end
        armEnableEdge(_asP)
        _asUid = tgt and tgt.uid or nil
        _asFp = nil
        if _asP and tgt then _asFp = findPrompt(tgt) end
        _G.JAF_StealTgt = tgt and { name = tgt.name, gen = tgt.genValue, genText = tgt.genText, mut = tgt.mutation } or nil
    end
    if _asP then
        if not _asPre and not stealPingGateReady(_asP, _asUid) then return end
        if _G.JAF_RagdollStealSync ~= false and not _asPre then
            local _ret = _ragUntil()
            if _ret > 0 then
                local _win = tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollPreArm) or ((Config.StealDuration or 1.3) - (tonumber(_G.JAF_RagdollLatePad) or 0.1))
                if (_ret - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) > _win then return end
            end end
        if stealNearest then executeNearest(_asP, _asUid) else executeSteal(_asP, _asUid, _asPre) end
    elseif _asUid and _G.JAF_StealPingDelay ~= false then
        local _pod = _podOf(_asUid)
        local _ba = _pod and _pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
        local _sp = _ba and _ba:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
        local _c = LocalPlayer.Character
        local _hr = _c and _c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not (_sp and _hr) then _pgKey = nil
        else
            local _spp, _hrp = _sp.Position, _hr.Position
            if _G.JAF_M2(_spp.X, _spp.Z, _hrp.X, _hrp.Z) > (Config.StealRadius or 95) then _pgKey = nil end
        end
    else
        _pgKey = nil
    end end)

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local target = resolvePriorityTarget()
        local prompt = target and findNearestPrompt(target)
        if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") or not prompt then stealBarHide(); return end
        if _barGui then _barGui.Enabled = true end
        if _barFill then
            _barFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(isAutoStealing and math.clamp((tick()-_stealStart)/math.max(tonumber(_G.JAF_StealCycleDur) or 1.3,0.1),0,1) or 0,1)
        end
    end)

    do
    -- Hit Recovery Back imported from Hub1; shared scanner and character access above.
    local PRIORITY_LIST = sourceConfig.PriorityList
    local isTpMoving = false
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local function SAB_LIVE() return LocalPlayer.Parent~=nil and sourceConfig.HitRecovery~=false end
    local function _P(instance,...)
        local args={...}
        for i=1,#args,2 do instance[args[i]]=args[i+1] end
    end
    local function getClosestBaseSign(part) return part end
    _G.JAF_F2UnderActive = function() return false end
    _G.JAF_GrappleBoostNow = function()
        if _G.SXE_FireGrapple then return _G.SXE_FireGrapple() end
    end
_G.JAF_HB = _G.JAF_HB or {}
_G.JAF_HB.width  = _G.JAF_HB.width  or 58
_G.JAF_HB.depth  = _G.JAF_HB.depth  or 88
_G.JAF_HB.height = _G.JAF_HB.height or 30
_G.JAF_HB.margin = _G.JAF_HB.margin or 28
_G.JAF_HB.podiumRange = _G.JAF_HB.podiumRange or 75
_G.JAF_HB.box = nil
do local _v = Workspace:FindFirstChild("JAF_HB_Viz"); if _v then pcall(function() 
_v:Destroy() end) end end
function _G.JAF_HB.clear() 
_G.JAF_HB.box = nil end
local _jafHbBuild = (function(signPart, petPos) 
    if not petPos then return end
    _G.JAF_HB.clear()
    local plot
    do
        local node = signPart
        while node and node ~= Workspace do
            if node:FindFirstChild("StealHitbox") or node:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums") then plot = node; break end
            node = node.Parent
        end end
    local mnx,mny,mnz =  math.huge, math.huge, math.huge
    local mxx,mxy,mxz = -math.huge,-math.huge,-math.huge
    local n = 0
    if plot then
        local _podMemo = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(plot:GetDescendants()) do
            if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Anchored and p.Transparency < 1 then
                local par  = p.Parent
                local skip = _podMemo[par]
                if skip == nil then
                    skip = false
                    local anc = par
                    while anc and anc ~= plot do if anc.Name == "AnimalPodiums" then skip = true; break end anc = anc.Parent end
                    if par ~= nil then _podMemo[par] = skip end
                end
                local q = not skip and p.Position or nil
                if q and math.abs(q.X) < 50000 and math.abs(q.Y) < 50000 and math.abs(q.Z) < 50000 then
                    local s = p.Size
                    local hx, hy, hz = s.X*0.5, s.Y*0.5, s.Z*0.5
                    local pcx,pcy,pcz, r00,r01,r02, r10,r11,r12, r20,r21,r22 = p.CFrame:GetComponents()
                    local ex = math.abs(r00)*hx + math.abs(r01)*hy + math.abs(r02)*hz
                    local ey = math.abs(r10)*hx + math.abs(r11)*hy + math.abs(r12)*hz
                    local ez = math.abs(r20)*hx + math.abs(r21)*hy + math.abs(r22)*hz
                    if pcx-ex < mnx then mnx = pcx-ex end
                    if pcx+ex > mxx then mxx = pcx+ex end
                    if pcy-ey < mny then mny = pcy-ey end
                    if pcy+ey > mxy then mxy = pcy+ey end
                    if pcz-ez < mnz then mnz = pcz-ez end
                    if pcz+ez > mxz then mxz = pcz+ez end
                    n = n + 1
                end end end end
    if n == 0 then
        local w, d, h = _G.JAF_HB.width or 58, _G.JAF_HB.depth or 88, _G.JAF_HB.height or 30
        local _f2u = _G.JAF_F2UnderActive and _G.JAF_F2UnderActive(petPos.Y)
        local fY = (petPos.Y > 10 and not _f2u)
            and (signPart and signPart:IsA("BasePart") and signPart.Position.Y + 4 or petPos.Y - 8)
            or -4.8
        _G.JAF_HB.box = { cf = CFrame.new(petPos.X, fY + h*0.5, petPos.Z), size = Vector3.new(w, h, d) }
        return
    end
    local m = _G.JAF_HB.margin or 3
    mnx, mxx, mnz, mxz = mnx-m, mxx+m, mnz-m, mxz+m
    _G.JAF_HB.box = { cf   = CFrame.new((mnx+mxx)*0.5, (mny+mxy)*0.5, (mnz+mxz)*0.5), size = Vector3.new(mxx-mnx, mxy-mny, mxz-mnz), }
end)
_G.JAF_HB.build = _jafHbBuild
function _G.JAF_HB.contains(pos) 
    local b = _G.JAF_HB.box
    if not b then return nil end
    local fps = _G.JAF_BaseFootprints
    if fps then
        local ax, az = b.cf.Position.X, b.cf.Position.Z
        for _, f in ipairs(fps) do
            if ax >= f.minX and ax <= f.maxX and az >= f.minZ and az <= f.maxZ then
                local xz   = _G.JAF_HB.gateXZ    or 4
                local up   = _G.JAF_HB.knockUp   or 15
                local down = _G.JAF_HB.knockDown or 6
                return pos.X >= f.minX - xz and pos.X <= f.maxX + xz
                       and pos.Z >= f.minZ - xz and pos.Z <= f.maxZ + xz
                       and pos.Y >= f.minY - down and pos.Y <= f.maxY + up
            end end end
    local rel = b.cf:PointToObjectSpace(pos)
    local h = b.size * 0.5
    return math.abs(rel.X) <= h.X and math.abs(rel.Y) <= h.Y and math.abs(rel.Z) <= h.Z
end
_G.JAF_BrainrotApproachSpeed = function() 
    local _cap = tonumber(_G.RoseCampSpeedCap)
    local function _ret(v) if _cap and _cap > 0 then return math.min(v, _cap) end return v end
    local _slider = math.clamp(tonumber(Config.TpSettings.BrainrotSpeed) or 175, 100, 500)
    if _G.JAF_BrainrotSpeedGate == false then return _ret(_slider) end
    local _floor = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.JAF_BrainrotNoBoostSpeed) or 170, 100, 500)
    if _slider <= _floor then return _ret(_slider) end
    local _c  = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _sa = _c and _c:GetAttribute("SpeedAllowance")
    if type(_sa) == "number" and _sa > 0 then return _ret(_slider) end
    return _ret(_floor)
end
_G.JAF_BrainrotSimpleOn = (function() 
    local _ov = _G.JAF_BrainrotSimpleTp
    local _on
    if _ov ~= nil then _on = (_ov ~= false) else _on = (Config.TpSettings.InstaTpBrainrot ~= false) end
    if not _on then return false end
    if _G.JAF_BrainrotSimpleRequireSA == false then return true end
    local _c  = LocalPlayer.Character
    local _sa = _c and _c:GetAttribute("SpeedAllowance")
    return type(_sa) == "number" and _sa > 0
end)
_G.JAF_BrainrotSimpleSnap = (function(hrp, dst, rotCF) 
    if not _G.JAF_BrainrotSimpleOn() then return false end
    if not (hrp and hrp.Parent and dst) then return true end
    if _G.SXE_HitRecoveryCancel or not SAB_LIVE() then
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        return true
    end
    local _snapWrite = function() 
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dst) * (rotCF or CFrame.new())
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        _G.JAF_BrainrotSnapT = tick()
    end
    local _tries  = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(_G.JAF_BrainrotSnapTries) or 2))
    local _verify = tonumber(_G.JAF_BrainrotSnapVerify); if _verify == nil then _verify = 0.05 end
    local _tol    = tonumber(_G.JAF_BrainrotSnapTol) or 3
    for _try = 1, _tries do
        _snapWrite()
        if _verify <= 0 then return true end
        local _v0 = tick()
        repeat
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            if _G.SXE_HitRecoveryCancel or not SAB_LIVE() or not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then return true end
        until tick() - _v0 >= _verify
        local _off = (hrp.Position - dst).Magnitude
        if _off <= _tol then return true end
    end
    if type(_G.JAF_BrainrotTweenOnly) == "function" then _G.JAF_BrainrotTweenOnly(hrp, dst, rotCF) end
    return true
end)
_G.JAF_BrainrotTweenOnly = (function(hrp, dst, rotCF) 
    for _ = 1, 6 do
        if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then return end
        local d = (hrp.Position - dst).Magnitude
        if d <= 0.5 then return end
        local speed = _G.JAF_BrainrotApproachSpeed()
        local tw = TweenService:Create(hrp,
            TweenInfo.new(math.clamp(d / speed, 0.05, 0.6), Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            {CFrame = CFrame.new(dst) * (rotCF or CFrame.new())})
        local done = false
        local conn = tw.Completed:Connect(function() 
done = true end)
        tw:Play()
        local changed = false
        while not done do
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) or not SAB_LIVE() then tw:Cancel(); conn:Disconnect(); return end
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            if _G.JAF_BrainrotApproachSpeed() ~= speed then tw:Cancel(); changed = true; break end
        end
        conn:Disconnect()
        if not changed then return end
    end end)
_G.JAF_BrainrotCFrameMove = function(hrp, dst, rotCF) 
    if _G.JAF_BrainrotSimpleSnap(hrp, dst, rotCF) then return end
    _G.JAF_BrainrotTweenOnly(hrp, dst, rotCF)
end
_G.JAF_TweenHrpBrainrot = function(hrp, dst, rotCF) 
    if not (hrp and hrp.Parent and dst) then return end
    if _G.JAF_BrainrotSimpleSnap(hrp, dst, rotCF) then return end
    if _G.JAF_BrainrotVelMove == false then return _G.JAF_BrainrotCFrameMove(hrp, dst, rotCF) end
    local _t0     = tick()
    local _budget = math.clamp((dst - hrp.Position).Magnitude / math.max(_G.JAF_BrainrotApproachSpeed(), 50) + 1.5, 1, 6)
    local _bestD, _stuckAt = (dst - hrp.Position).Magnitude, tick()
    local _dt = 1/60
    local _char0 = LocalPlayer.Character
    while tick() - _t0 < _budget do
        if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then return end
        if _G.SXE_HitRecoveryCancel or not SAB_LIVE() or LocalPlayer.Character ~= _char0 then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            return
        end
        local _pos  = hrp.Position
        local _diff = dst - _pos
        local _dist = _diff.Magnitude
        if _dist <= 1 then break end
        local _speed = _G.JAF_BrainrotApproachSpeed()
        if _G.JAF_BrainrotLikeFlight ~= false and Config.TpSettings.TpMode == "grapple"
           and _G.JAF_GrappleTick and _G.JAF_GrappleSpeedCap then
            _G.JAF_GrappleTick()
            _speed = math.min(_speed, _G.JAF_GrappleSpeedCap())
        end
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        if _dist > 8 then
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _diff.Unit * _speed
        else
            local _stepVec = _diff.Unit * math.min(_speed * _dt, _dist * 0.45)
            if _G.JAF_BrainrotCloseInVel == false then
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            else
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _stepVec / math.max(_dt, 1/240)
            end
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(_pos + _stepVec) * (rotCF or CFrame.new())
        end
        if _dist < _bestD - 0.5 then
            _bestD, _stuckAt = _dist, tick()
        elseif tick() - _stuckAt > (tonumber(_G.JAF_BrainrotStallSecs) or 0.35) then
            _G.JAF_BrainrotCFrameMove(hrp, dst, rotCF)
            break
        end
        _dt = RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
    if hrp and hrp.Parent then
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        if (dst - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= 8 then hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dst) * (rotCF or CFrame.new()) end
    end end

_G.JAF_CreateF3Platform = function(position) 
    if not position then return end
    for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do if c.Name == "XiTempPlatform" then pcall(function() 
c:Destroy() end) end end
    local plat = Instance.new("Part")
    _P(plat, "Name", "XiTempPlatform", "Size", Vector3.new(3, 1, 3), "Position", position, "Color", Color3.new(1, 0, 0), "Material", Enum.Material.Neon, "Anchored", true, "CanCollide", true, "Transparency", 0.3, "Parent", Workspace)
    task.spawn(function() 
task.wait(tonumber(_G.JAF_F3PlatformMaxLife) or 30); while plat.Parent and game.Players.LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true do task.wait(0.5) end; pcall(function() 
plat:Destroy() end) end)
    return plat
end

end

local _findRetry; _findRetry = function(a) 
    for _try = 1, (_G.JAF_RecArmedRetries or 8) do if findAdorneeGlobal(a) then return true end RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
end
LocalPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function() 
    if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") then
        pcall(_G.JAF_DestroyAllF3Platforms)
        local _, hrp2 = getChar()
        if not hrp2 then _wasStealingHit = false; return end
        local tgt2 = manualTarget or SharedState._currentTpTarget
        if tgt2 and tgt2.owner == LocalPlayer.Name then tgt2 = nil end
        if not tgt2 then
            local _aBR, _aBV = math.huge, -math.huge
            local _aUP = (Config.AutoTPPriority ~= false) and type(PRIORITY_LIST) == "table"
            for _, a in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) do
                if a and a.owner ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
                    if _aUP then
                        local _aRk, _aV = _G.JAF_PrioScore(a), tonumber(a.genValue) or 0
                        if _aRk < _aBR or (_aRk == _aBR and _aV > _aBV) then _aBR, _aBV, tgt2 = _aRk, _aV, a end
                    elseif not tgt2 then tgt2 = a end
                end end end
        local part2 = tgt2 and findAdorneeGlobal(tgt2)
        if part2 then
            _hbBuildAt(part2)
            SharedState._stealFloorY = part2.Position.Y
            local _, _, hum2 = getChar()
            local _alive2 = hum2 and hum2.Health > 0 and LocalPlayer.Character == (hrp2.Parent)
            local inBox = _G.JAF_HB.contains(hrp2.Position) == true
            _wasStealingHit = (_alive2 and inBox) or false
            SharedState._armedRecoveryTarget = _wasStealingHit and tgt2 or nil
            SharedState._armedRecoveryPos    = _wasStealingHit and part2.Position or nil
        else
            _wasStealingHit = false
            SharedState._armedRecoveryTarget, SharedState._armedRecoveryPos = nil, nil
        end
        return
    end
    local _afterDrop = false
    local _tok = _G.JAF_DropRecToken
    if _tok then
        _G.JAF_DropRecToken = nil
        if tick() < _tok then
            _afterDrop = true
            local _t0 = tick()
            while type(_G.JAF_DropBrainrotActive) == "function" and _G.JAF_DropBrainrotActive() and tick() - _t0 < (tonumber(_G.JAF_DropRecoverWaitMax) or 8) do
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            local _sT   = tick()
            local _sMax = tonumber(_G.JAF_DropRecoverSettleMax) or 4
            local _vCap = tonumber(_G.JAF_DropRecoverSettleVel) or 40
            local _spd, _grounded = -1, false
            while tick() - _sT < _sMax do
                local _, _hs, _hums = getChar()
                if not _hs then break end
                _spd = _hs.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
                _grounded = (_hums ~= nil) and _hums.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air
                if _spd <= _vCap and _grounded then break end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end
            local _, _hz = getChar()
            if _hz then _hz.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero; _hz.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
        end end
    if not _wasStealingHit then return end
    _wasStealingHit = false
    local _, _h9, _hum9 = getChar()
    local _orph = _orphHRP()
    if not Config.HitRecovery then return end
    if not SAB_LIVE() then return end
    if isTpMoving or _G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
    if _G.JAF_AutoTurretActive and _G.JAF_AutoTurretActive() and _G.JAF_DestroyTurretIfThreat then pcall(_G.JAF_DestroyTurretIfThreat) end
    if _G.JAF_HitRecCooldownUntil and tick() < _G.JAF_HitRecCooldownUntil then return end
    if _G.RecoveryInProgress then return end
    if _G.invisibleStealEnabled or _orphHRP() then
        if _G.JAF_RecoverDuringInvis == false then return end
        pcall(function() 
if _G.SXE_StopInvisSteal then _G.SXE_StopInvisSteal() end end)
        for _ = 1, (tonumber(_G.JAF_RecoverInvisRevertFrames) or 4) do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
    end
    if _orphHRP() then return end
    if _G.JAF_RecLastRun and tick() - _G.JAF_RecLastRun < 1.5 then return end
    _G.JAF_RecLastRun = tick()
    local c, hrp, hum = getChar()
    if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or not hrp.Parent then pcall(_G.JAF_HB.clear); return end
    local target = manualTarget
    if not target and _G.JAF_HitRecFollowBest ~= false then
        local best = _G.JAF_ResolveBest and _G.JAF_ResolveBest()
        if best and best.owner ~= LocalPlayer.Name and _findRetry(best) then target = best end
    end
    local armed = not target and SharedState._armedRecoveryTarget
    if armed and armed.owner ~= LocalPlayer.Name and _findRetry(armed) then target = armed end
    local cur = not target and SharedState._currentTpTarget
    if cur and cur.owner ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
        local _cp = findAdorneeGlobal(cur)
        local _aY = (SharedState._armedRecoveryPos and SharedState._armedRecoveryPos.Y) or SharedState._stealFloorY
        if _cp and (not _aY or math.abs(_cp.Position.Y - _aY) <= (_G.JAF_RecoverFloorBand or 12)) then target = cur end
    end
    if not target then
        local _, _rhrp = getChar()
        local _refY = (SharedState._armedRecoveryPos and SharedState._armedRecoveryPos.Y) or SharedState._stealFloorY
        if _rhrp and _refY then
            local _band, _range = _G.JAF_RecoverFloorBand or 12, (_G.JAF_HB.podiumRange or 75)
            local bestV, _bestRank = -1, math.huge
            local _usePrioRec = (Config.AutoTPPriority ~= false) and type(PRIORITY_LIST) == "table"
            for _, a in ipairs(SharedState.AllAnimalsCache) do
                if a and a.owner ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
                    local prt = findAdorneeGlobal(a)
                    if prt and math.abs(prt.Position.Y - _refY) <= _band and (prt.Position - _rhrp.Position).Magnitude <= _range then
                        local v = tonumber(a.genValue) or 0
                        if _usePrioRec then
                            local _rank = _G.JAF_PrioScore(a)
                            if _rank < _bestRank or (_rank == _bestRank and v > bestV) then _bestRank, bestV, target = _rank, v, a end
                        elseif v > bestV then bestV = v; target = a end
                    end end end end end
    if not target then return end
    local part = findAdorneeGlobal(target)
    _hbBuildAt(part)
    if not part then return end
    local petPos = part.Position
    local _raw = _G.JAF_HB.contains(hrp.Position)
    if _raw ~= true then
        local _dd = (hrp.Position - petPos).Magnitude
        local _cap = tonumber(_G.JAF_DropRecoverMaxDist) or 400
        if not (_afterDrop and _dd <= _cap) then return end
    end
    local MAX = tonumber(_G.JAF_RecMaxRetries) or 3
    if tick() - (_G.JAF_RecWindowStart or 0) > (tonumber(_G.JAF_RecRetryWindow) or 8) then _G.JAF_RecConsecutive = 0; _G.JAF_RecWindowStart = tick() end
    _G.JAF_RecConsecutive = (_G.JAF_RecConsecutive or 0) + 1
    if _G.JAF_RecConsecutive > MAX then pcall(_G.JAF_HB.clear) return end
    _G.JAF_HitRecCooldownUntil = tick() + 1.5
    _G.RecoveryInProgress = true
    _G.SXE_HitRecoveryCancel = false
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
        isTpMoving = true; _G.SXE_HitRecoveryActive=true; _G.JAF_TpActive=true
        _G.JAF_TpGen = (_G.JAF_TpGen or 0) + 1
        local _, _hrH0 = getChar()
        local _hrP0 = _hrH0 and _hrH0.Position
        local snapChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        local snapAlive; snapAlive = function() 
            if _G.SXE_HitRecoveryCancel then return false end
            return LocalPlayer.Character == snapChar and snapChar ~= nil
        end
        _G.SXE_EquipCarpet()
        task.wait(0.01)
        if target.isWalking then
            local LERP_SPEED, HOVER_HEIGHT = 0.35, 3
            local anchorDone, lastGoodPos, missedFrames = false, nil, 0
            local done = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
            task.delay(tonumber(_G.JAF_WalkRecoverMax) or 10, function() 
if not anchorDone then anchorDone = true; done:Fire() end end)
            local conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt) 
                if anchorDone then return end
                if not snapAlive() then anchorDone=true; done:Fire(); return end
                c, hrp = getChar()
                if not hrp then return end
                local currentPart = findAdorneeGlobal(target)
                if not currentPart or not target.model or not target.model.Parent then
                    missedFrames = missedFrames + 1
                    if missedFrames >= 90 then anchorDone=true; done:Fire(); return end
                    if lastGoodPos then
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(lastGoodPos + Vector3.new(0, HOVER_HEIGHT, 0)), LERP_SPEED)
                    end
                    return
                end
                missedFrames = 0
                lastGoodPos = currentPart.Position
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(lastGoodPos + Vector3.new(0, HOVER_HEIGHT, 0)), LERP_SPEED)
            end)
            done.Event:Wait()
            done:Destroy()
            conn:Disconnect()
            isTpMoving = false; _G.SXE_HitRecoveryActive=false; _G.JAF_TpActive=false
            return
        end
        local isUnderF2 = _G.JAF_F2UnderActive(petPos.Y)
        local snapY = (petPos.Y > 22) and (petPos.Y - 8) or isUnderF2 and (petPos.Y - (tonumber(_G.JAF_F2UnderDrop) or 8)) or (petPos.Y + 2.5)
        local snapPos = Vector3.new(petPos.X, snapY, petPos.Z)
        if (petPos.Y - (hrp and hrp.Position.Y or 0)) > 2 or isUnderF2 then
            if _G.JAF_CreateF3Platform then pcall(_G.JAF_CreateF3Platform, snapPos - Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)) end
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        _G.SXE_EquipCarpet(); task.wait(0.01)
        c, hrp = getChar()
        if hrp and snapAlive() then
            local _useMover = _G.JAF_HitRecUseMover ~= false and type(_G.JAF_TweenHrpBrainrot) == "function"
            for _attempt = 1, 2 do
                if not snapAlive() then break end
                c, hrp = getChar(); if not hrp then break end
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                if _useMover then
                    if type(_G.JAF_GrappleBoostNow) == "function" then pcall(_G.JAF_GrappleBoostNow, snapPos) end
                    _G.SXE_EquipCarpet()
                    pcall(_G.JAF_TweenHrpBrainrot, hrp, snapPos, nil)
                end
                c, hrp = getChar(); if not hrp then break end
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                if (hrp.Position - snapPos).Magnitude <= 8 then break end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end end
        local _, _h8 = getChar()
        local _end = _h8 and _h8.Position
        local _orph8 = _orphHRP()
        pcall(_G.JAF_HB.clear)
        isTpMoving = false; _G.SXE_HitRecoveryActive=false; _G.JAF_TpActive=false
        end)
        isTpMoving = false
        _G.SXE_HitRecoveryActive = false
        _G.JAF_TpActive = false
        _G.RecoveryInProgress = false
        pcall(_G.JAF_HB.clear)
        if not ok then warn("[Hit Recovery Back] "..tostring(err)) end
    end)
end)
    end
    end
    local function preFlyStrip(plotName)
        if plotName then
            pcall(_G.SXE_LockPrioPlot, plotName)
        end
    end
    local function _doVelocityTPImpl(pre)
        if isTeleporting and (os.clock() - _tpStartedAt) < 30 then
            return
        end
        isTeleporting = true
        _G.__SXE_TPStarted = true
        _tpStartedAt = os.clock()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then
            isTeleporting = false
            _G.__SXE_TPStarted = nil
            return
        end
        local allPets
        if pre==nil and _G.SXE_SelectedStealTarget and _G.SXE_StealTargetAPI
            and _G.SXE_StealTargetAPI.alive(_G.SXE_SelectedStealTarget) then
            pre=_G.SXE_SelectedStealTarget
        end
        if type(pre) == "table" and (pre.plot or pre.name or pre.index) then
            allPets = { pre }
        elseif type(pre) == "table" and #pre > 0 then
            allPets = pre
        else
            allPets = scanAllPets()
            if #allPets == 0 then
                local _t0 = os.clock()
                while #allPets == 0 and (os.clock() - _t0) < 2 do
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    allPets = scanAllPets()
                end
            end
        end
        if #allPets == 0 then
            isTeleporting = false
            return
        end
        local pet = _pickByMode(allPets) or allPets[1]
        armSteal(pet)
        pcall(_ensurePetPos, pet)
        local petPos = pet.position or pet.plotHint
        if not petPos then
            local _t0 = os.clock()
            repeat
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                pcall(_ensurePetPos, pet)
                petPos = pet.position or pet.plotHint
                if not petPos then
                    for i = 1, #allPets do
                        local cand = allPets[i]
                        pcall(_ensurePetPos, cand)
                        local pos = cand.position or cand.plotHint
                        if pos then
                            pet = cand
                            petPos = pos
                            armSteal(pet)
                            break
                        end
                    end
                end
            until petPos or (os.clock() - _t0) > 2
        end
        local _tpSpd = _G.D3GovClamp(Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.GrabbleTPSpeed, petPos)
        local petName = pet.name
        if not petPos then
            isTeleporting = false
            _G.__SXE_TPStarted = nil
            return
        end
        -- Every TP uses the same grapple/flight-tool handshake, including manual picks.
        _G.__SXE_GrappleFired = false
        if type(_G.SXE_CarpetEngage) ~= "function" then return end
        local engageOK, flightTool = pcall(_G.SXE_CarpetEngage)
        local equippedCharacter = LP.Character
        if not engageOK or not _G.__SXE_GrappleFired or not flightTool
            or not equippedCharacter or not equippedCharacter:FindFirstChild(flightTool) then
            return
        end
        if petPos.Y <= 8.9 and isPlotUnlocked(pet.plot) then
            pcall(_G.SXE_EquipCarpet)
            local _to = Vector3.new(petPos.X, FRONT_Y_LOW, petPos.Z)
            local _faceDir
            do
                local idx = getClosestBaseIdx(petPos)
                local _, frontFace = buildFrontCandidate(idx, false, hrp.Position.Z)
                _faceDir = frontFace
            end
            preFlyStrip(pet.plot)
            _G.__SXE_VelFlying = true
            local route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, _to, _faceDir)
            if not route or #route == 0 then route = { _to } end
            velMoveThrough(hrp, route, _tpSpd)
            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
            isTeleporting = false
            return
        end
        pcall(_G.SXE_EquipCarpet)
        local destPos, facingDir = resolveCloneDest(hrp.Position, petPos, petName)
        if facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            destPos = wallFrontPos(destPos, facingDir)
        end
        preFlyStrip(pet.plot)
        _G.__SXE_VelFlying = true
        local _route = computeRoute(hrp.Position, destPos, facingDir)
        if not _route or #_route == 0 then _route = { destPos } end
        local _stepped = {}
        do
            local startY = hrp.Position.Y
            local destY = destPos.Y
            local prev = hrp.Position
            local totalFlat = 0
            for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do
                totalFlat = totalFlat + Vector3.new(wp.X - prev.X, 0, wp.Z - prev.Z).Magnitude
                prev = wp
            end
            if totalFlat < 0.01 then totalFlat = 0.01 end
            prev = hrp.Position
            local travelled = 0
            for _, wp in ipairs(_route) do
                travelled = travelled + Vector3.new(wp.X - prev.X, 0, wp.Z - prev.Z).Magnitude
                local rampY = startY + (destY - startY) * (travelled / totalFlat)
                _stepped[#_stepped + 1] = Vector3.new(wp.X, rampY, wp.Z)
                prev = wp
            end
            if #_stepped > 0 then _stepped[#_stepped] = destPos end
        end
        velMoveThrough(hrp, _stepped, _tpSpd)
        local look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        if facingDir and facingDir.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            look = Vector3.new(facingDir.X, 0, facingDir.Z)
            if look.Magnitude < 0.1 then look = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) else look = look.Unit end
        end
        local hitName
        destPos, hitName = wallFrontPos(destPos, facingDir)
        do
            local into = 0.45
            local ln = string.lower(hitName or "")
            if string.find(ln, "laser", 1, true) then into = 0 end
            destPos = destPos + look * into
        end
        hrp.Anchored = false
        local function _faceLook()
            if look.Magnitude > 0.1 and hrp and hrp.Parent then
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + look)
            end
        end
        _faceLook()
        vZero(hrp)
        local _stopFall = _antiFall(hrp, destPos)
        armSteal(pet)
        local _cloneDelay = tonumber(Config and Config.TpSettings and Config.TpSettings.CloneDelayVal) or 0.27
        if _cloneDelay > 0 then
            task.wait(_cloneDelay)
        end
        local _cloneOk = doClone()
        if _stopFall then pcall(_stopFall, false) end
        if _cloneOk then
            _G.isCloning = false
            task.wait(0.3)
            goToBrainrot(petPos)
        end
    end
    function doVelocityTP(pre)
        if _G.RecoveryInProgress then return false end
        if isTeleporting and (os.clock() - _tpStartedAt) < 30 then return false end
        pcall(_doVelocityTPImpl, pre)
        isTeleporting = false
        _G.__SXE_TPStarted = nil
        _G.__SXE_VelFlying = false
        task.delay(1.2, function()
            if not (_G.__SXE_VelFlying or _G.__SXE_TPStarted) then
                pcall(_G.SXE_FlushKills)
            end
        end)
    end
    _G.SXE_DoVelocityTP = doVelocityTP
    local function stealNearestPrio()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        local origin = hrp.Position
        local cache = {}
        local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
        local isKey = _G.SXE_IsPlotChanKey
        local reg = _G.XenSyncAll and _G.XenSyncAll()
        if type(reg) ~= "table" then return end
        for name, ch in next, reg do
            if not (isKey and isKey(name)) then continue end
            if not ch or isMyPlot(ch) then continue end
            local list = channelGet(ch, "AnimalList")
            if type(list) ~= "table" then continue end
            local bestMutOnPlot = {}
            for slot, animalData in pairs(list) do
                if type(animalData) ~= "table" then continue end
                local animalName = animalData.Index
                if type(animalName) ~= "string" then continue end
                if not _prioRank[string.lower(animalName)] then continue end
                if _ANGVELSIsFusing(animalData) then continue end
                local mut = _adMut(animalData)
                local key = string.lower(animalName)
                local prev = bestMutOnPlot[key]
                if not prev or _mutRank(mut) < _mutRank(prev.mutation) then
                    bestMutOnPlot[key] = {
                        name = animalName, index = animalName, mutation = mut,
                        plot = tostring(name), slot = tostring(slot),
                    }
                end
            end
            for _, pet in pairs(bestMutOnPlot) do
                _ensurePetPos(pet)
                local pos = pet.position or pet.plotHint
                if not pos then continue end
                cache[#cache + 1] = { pet = pet, pos = pos }
                local d = (pos - origin).Magnitude
                if d < bestD then
                    bestD, best = d, pet
                end
            end
        end
        if not best then
            return
        end
        _G.SXE_SelectedStealTarget=nil
        _nearestCache = cache
        _nearestAt = origin
        _nearestOn = true
        armSteal(best)
        pcall(_G.SXE_LockPrioPlot, best.plot)
        nearestPaint(true)
    end
    _G.SXE_StealNearestPrio = stealNearestPrio
end

do
    local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local pg = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 8)
    if not pg then
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
    local old = pg:FindFirstChild("SXENearest")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    local g = Instance.new("ScreenGui")

    panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18)

    panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    panel.Parent = g
    local pc = Instance.new("UICorner")

    lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pad.Parent = panel

    if controlLayout then

        if tonumber(v[1]) and tonumber(v[2]) and tonumber(v[3]) and tonumber(v[4]) then
            panel.Position = UDim2.new(v[1],v[2],v[3],v[4])
        end
