    panel.Active = true
    panel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            moving, origin, originPos = true, input.Position, panel.Position
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if moving and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            local d = input.Position-origin
            panel.Position = UDim2.new(originPos.X.Scale,originPos.X.Offset+d.X,originPos.Y.Scale,originPos.Y.Offset+d.Y)
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if moving and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            moving = false
            local v = panel.Position
            _G.SXE_SaveControls({v.X.Scale,v.X.Offset,v.Y.Scale,v.Y.Offset})
        end
    end)
    local OFF = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28)

    local buttonOrder = 10

    local sliderOrder = 0
    local function mkSlider(fmt, lo, hi, init, cb)
        local hold = Instance.new("Frame")
        hold.LayoutOrder = sliderOrder; sliderOrder = sliderOrder + 1
        hold.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
        hold.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hold.Parent = panel
        local lab = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lab.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
        lab.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lab.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
        lab.TextSize = 11
        lab.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lab.Text = string.format(fmt, init)
        lab.Parent = hold
        local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
        bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 14)
        bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 16)
        bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
        bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar.Parent = hold
        Instance.new("UICorner", bar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
        fill.Size = UDim2.new((init - lo) / (hi - lo), 0, 1, 0)
        fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 120)
        fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        fill.Parent = bar
        Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local dragging = false
        local function update(input)
            local x = math.clamp((input.Position.X - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / bar.AbsoluteSize.X, 0, 1)
            local val = lo + x * (hi - lo)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(x, 0, 1, 0)
            lab.Text = string.format(fmt, val)
            cb(val)
        end
        bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true; update(i)
            end
        end)
        UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then update(i) end
        end)
        UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false; _G.SXE_SaveControls() end
        end)
        return hold, function(v)
            fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp((v-lo)/(hi-lo),0,1),1)
            lab.Text = string.format(fmt,v)
        end
    end
    mkSlider("Invis depth: %.1f", 0, 10, tonumber(Config.InvisibleStealDepth) or 7, function(v)
        Config.InvisibleStealDepth = v
    end)
    local rotHolder, refreshRotation = mkSlider("Invis rot: %.0f\194\176", 0, 360, tonumber(Config.InvisibleStealRotation) or 180, function(v)
        Config.InvisibleStealRotation = math.floor(v + 0.5)
    end)
    local rotButtons = Instance.new("Frame")
    rotButtons.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,26)
    rotButtons.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    rotButtons.LayoutOrder = 2
    rotButtons.Parent = panel
    for i, angle in ipairs({180,220}) do
        local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
        button.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-3,1,0)
        button.Position = UDim2.new((i-1)*0.5,i==1 and 0 or 3,0,0)
        button.BackgroundColor3 = OFF
        button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
        button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        button.TextSize = 11
        button.Text = tostring(angle)
        button.Parent = rotButtons
        Instance.new("UICorner",button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,6)
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Config.InvisibleStealRotation = angle
            refreshRotation(angle)
            _G.SXE_SaveControls()
        end)
    end

    local spdHold = Instance.new("Frame")
    spdHold.LayoutOrder = 3
    spdHold.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
    spdHold.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    spdHold.Parent = panel
    local spdLab = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    spdLab.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
    spdLab.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    spdLab.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 220)
    spdLab.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    spdLab.TextSize = 10
    spdLab.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    spdLab.Parent = spdHold
    local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
    bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 8)
    bar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20)
    bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50)
    bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bar.Parent = spdHold
    local bc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    bc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    bc.Parent = bar
    local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
    fill.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
    fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fill.Parent = bar
    local fc = Instance.new("UICorner")
    fc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    fc.Parent = fill
    local SPD_MIN, SPD_MAX = 60, 250
    local function setSpdUi(v)
        v = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 140, SPD_MIN, SPD_MAX)
        Config.WaveriderSpeed = v
        local rel = (v - SPD_MIN) / (SPD_MAX - SPD_MIN)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 1, 0)
        spdLab.Text = "Wave speed: " .. tostring(math.floor(v + 0.5))
    end
    setSpdUi(Config.WaveriderSpeed)
    local drag = false
    local function fromX(x)
        local rel = math.clamp((x - bar.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, bar.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
        setSpdUi(SPD_MIN + (SPD_MAX - SPD_MIN) * rel)
    end
    bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            drag = true
            fromX(i.Position.X)
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            drag = false
            _G.SXE_SaveControls()
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if drag and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
            fromX(i.Position.X)
        end
    end)

    paint(bPs, "KICK TO PS", Config.KickToPS == true)
    paint(bJump, "INFINITE JUMP", Config.InfiniteJump == true)
    paint(bWave, "WAVERIDER SPEED (A)", Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled == true)
    paint(bClone, "AUTO CLONE (V)", Config.AutoClone ~= false)
    paint(bInv, "INV STEAL", Config.InvSteal ~= false)

    _G.SXE_RefreshControlButtons = function()
        paint(bInv, "INV STEAL", Config.InvSteal ~= false)
        paint(bClone, "AUTO CLONE (V)", Config.AutoClone ~= false)
        paint(bWave, "WAVERIDER SPEED", Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled == true)
    end
    local PS_PLACE_ID = 109983668079237
    local PS_LINK_CODE = "85781667033108162011177105658744"
    local autoKickFired = false
    local function doAutoKick()
        if autoKickFired then return end
        autoKickFired = true
        if not pcall(function()
            game:GetService("ExperienceService"):LaunchExperience({ placeId = PS_PLACE_ID, linkCode = PS_LINK_CODE })
        end) then
            pcall(function() LocalPlayer:Kick("\nBIR PRO") end)
        end
    end
    local function startAutoKick()
        local pgui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not pgui then return end
        if _G.__SXE_PsKickConn then
            pcall(function() _G.__SXE_PsKickConn:Disconnect() end)
            _G.__SXE_PsKickConn = nil
        end
        _G.__SXE_PsKickConn = pgui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(gui)
            if not Config.KickToPS or autoKickFired then return end
            local txt = (gui:IsA("TextLabel") or gui:IsA("TextButton")) and gui.Text
            if txt and string.find(txt, "You stole") then
                doAutoKick()
            end
        end)
    end
    local function stopAutoKick()
        if _G.__SXE_PsKickConn then
            pcall(function() _G.__SXE_PsKickConn:Disconnect() end)
            _G.__SXE_PsKickConn = nil
        end
    end

    local waveConn
    local function setWaveriderSpeed(enabled)
        Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled = enabled
        if _G.SXE_SaveControls then _G.SXE_SaveControls() end
        if waveConn then
            waveConn:Disconnect()
            waveConn = nil
        end
        if not enabled then return end
        local lastTick = 0
        waveConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if _G.__SXE_VelFlying or _G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.isCloning or _G.RecoveryInProgress then return end
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastTick < 0.066 then return end
            lastTick = now
            local c = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not c then return end
            local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not hrp then return end
            local bp=LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
            local tool=_G.SXE_FindFlightTool and _G.SXE_FindFlightTool(c,bp)
            if not tool then return end
            if tool.Parent~=c and _G.__SXE_GrappleFired then
                pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(tool) end)
            end
            if tool.Parent~=c then return end
            local spd = tonumber(Config.WaveriderSpeed) or 140
            local md = hum.MoveDirection
            if md.Magnitude > 0 then
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(md.X * spd, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, md.Z * spd)
            else
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
            end
        end)
    end

    _G.SXE_SetWaveSpeed = function(on)
        setWaveriderSpeed(on)
        paint(bWave, "WAVERIDER SPEED", Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled)
    end
    local infJumpConn, lastJump = nil, 0
    local function setInfiniteJump(enabled)
        Config.InfiniteJump = enabled
        if _G.SXE_SaveControls then _G.SXE_SaveControls() end
        if infJumpConn then
            infJumpConn:Disconnect()
            infJumpConn = nil
        end
        if not enabled then return end
        infJumpConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if _G.__SXE_VelFlying or _G.__SXE_TPStarted then return end
            if not UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then return end
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastJump < 0.1 then return end
            local c = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not c then return end
            local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
            lastJump = now
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 55, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
        end)
    end

    bPs.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Config.KickToPS = not Config.KickToPS
        _G.SXE_SaveControls()
        paint(bPs, "KICK TO PS", Config.KickToPS)
        if Config.KickToPS then startAutoKick() else stopAutoKick() end
    end)
    bJump.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Config.InfiniteJump = not Config.InfiniteJump
        paint(bJump, "INFINITE JUMP", Config.InfiniteJump)
        setInfiniteJump(Config.InfiniteJump)
    end)
    local function toggleWave()
        Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled = not Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled
        paint(bWave, "WAVERIDER SPEED (A)", Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled)
        setWaveriderSpeed(Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled)
    end
    bWave.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleWave)
    bClone.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Config.AutoClone = not (Config.AutoClone ~= false)
        _G.SXE_SaveControls()
        paint(bClone, "AUTO CLONE (V)", Config.AutoClone)
    end)
    bInv.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Config.InvSteal = not (Config.InvSteal ~= false)
        _G.SXE_SaveControls()
        paint(bInv, "INV STEAL", Config.InvSteal)
        if not Config.InvSteal and _G.SXE_StopInvisSteal then
            pcall(_G.SXE_StopInvisSteal)
        elseif Config.InvSteal and _G.SXE_ResetInvisSteal then
            pcall(_G.SXE_ResetInvisSteal)
        elseif _G.SXE_InvStealSet then
            pcall(_G.SXE_InvStealSet, Config.InvSteal)
        end
    end)
    if Config.KickToPS then startAutoKick() end
    if Config.InfiniteJump then setInfiniteJump(true) end
    if Config.WaveriderSpeedEnabled then setWaveriderSpeed(true) end

    UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
        if processed or _G.SXE_KeybindActionsActive then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.X and _G.TacoInstaReset then
            task.spawn(_G.TacoInstaReset)
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.V and Config.AutoClone ~= false then
            task.spawn(instantClone)
        -- AZERTY: KeyCode.Q corresponds to the physical A key.
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Q then
            toggleWave()
        end
    end)
    end)  -- close task.spawn panel delay
end

task.spawn(function()
    local t0 = os.clock()
    while os.clock() - t0 < 12 do
        if _G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.__SXE_VelFlying then return end
        local ok, pets = pcall(_G.SXE_ScanPets)
        if ok and type(pets) == "table" and #pets > 0 then
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp and _G.SXE_DoVelocityTP then
                pcall(_G.SXE_DoVelocityTP, pets)
                return
            end
        end
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
end)

do
    local LP = LocalPlayer
    local hard = false
    local humRef = nil
    local n = 0
    local stealing = LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
    LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        stealing = LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
    end)
    local function want()
        return _G.__SXE_VelFlying or _G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.isCloning or stealing
    end
    local function protect()
        if not want() then
            if hard then
                hard = false
                local hum = humRef
                if hum and hum.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true)
                        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true)
                        hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
                    end)
                end
                humRef = nil
            end
            return
        end
        n += 1
        local hum = humRef
        if not (hum and hum.Parent) then
            local char = LP.Character
            hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return end
            humRef = hum
        end
        if not hard then
            hard = true
            pcall(function()
                hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
                hum.RequiresNeck = false
                hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
                hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false)
                hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
            end)
        end
        if n % 3 ~= 0 then return end
        if hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth then
            hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
        end
    end
    RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(protect)
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        hard = false
        humRef = nil
    end)
end

task.delay(10, function()
    local XWorkspace = Workspace
    local on = false
    local orig = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
    local conns = {}
    local loopId = 0
    local ALPHA = tonumber(_G.XRayAlpha) or 0.5
    local FOLDERS = { "Base", "PlotSign", "FriendPanel", "Cash", "Laser", "Decorations", "Skin", "Unlock", "Purchases" }

    local function setTransparency(instance, alpha, id)
        if not instance then return end
        if id ~= loopId then return end
        local function apply(obj)
            if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                if orig[obj] == nil then
                    if obj.Transparency == alpha then orig[obj] = 0
                    else orig[obj] = obj.Transparency end
                end
                local o = orig[obj]
                if o < 1 then
                    local target = o + (1 - o) * alpha
                    if math.abs(obj.Transparency - target) > 0.01 then obj.Transparency = target end
                end
            end
        end
        apply(instance)
        for i, child in ipairs(instance:GetDescendants()) do
            apply(child)
            if i % 20 == 0 then
                task.wait()
                if id ~= loopId then return end
            end
        end
    end
    local function trackSubtree(root, alpha, id)
        if not root or id ~= loopId then return end
        setTransparency(root, alpha, id)
        if id ~= loopId then return end
        conns[#conns + 1] = root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if id ~= loopId then return end
            setTransparency(obj, alpha, id)
        end)
    end
    local function processPlot(plot, alpha, id)
        if not plot or id ~= loopId then return end
        for _, fname in ipairs(FOLDERS) do
            if id ~= loopId then return end
            trackSubtree(plot:FindFirstChild(fname), alpha, id)
        end
        if id ~= loopId then return end
        conns[#conns + 1] = plot.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if id ~= loopId then return end
            for _, fname in ipairs(FOLDERS) do
                if child.Name == fname then trackSubtree(child, alpha, id); break end
            end
        end)
        local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
        if podiums then
            local function processPodium(podium)
                for _, child in ipairs(podium:GetChildren()) do
                    if child.Name == "Claim" then
                        trackSubtree(child, alpha, id)
                    elseif child.Name == "Base" then
                        trackSubtree(child:FindFirstChild("Decorations"), alpha, id)
                    elseif child:IsA("Model") and child.Name ~= "Decorations" then
                        trackSubtree(child, alpha, id)
                    end
                end
            end
            for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do processPodium(podium) end
            conns[#conns + 1] = podiums.ChildAdded:Connect(function(podium)
                if id ~= loopId then return end
                task.wait(0.1)
                if id ~= loopId then return end
                processPodium(podium)
            end)
        end
    end
    local function applyAllPlots(alpha, id)
        local plotsFolder = XWorkspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if not plotsFolder then return end
        for _, plot in ipairs(plotsFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if id ~= loopId then return end
            processPlot(plot, alpha, id)
            task.wait()
        end
        conns[#conns + 1] = plotsFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(plot)
            if id ~= loopId then return end
            task.wait(0.2)
            processPlot(plot, alpha, id)
        end)
    end
    local function enable()
        for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
            if typeof(c) == "RBXScriptConnection" then c:Disconnect() end
        end
        conns = {}
        loopId = loopId + 1
        local id = loopId
        on = true
        task.spawn(function()
            while id == loopId and not XWorkspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") do task.wait(0.2) end
            if id ~= loopId then return end
            pcall(applyAllPlots, ALPHA, id)
        end)
    end
    _G.XRayEnabled = true
    _G.setXRay = function(enabled)
        ALPHA = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.XRayAlpha) or 0.5, 0, 1)
        _G.XRayEnabled = enabled == true
        if enabled then enable() end
    end
    enable()
end)

do
    local LP = LocalPlayer
    local RS = RunService
    local active, start, stop, armed = false, nil, nil, false
    local d, a = 7, 180

    local function boot()
        if start then return end
        local conns = {}
        local realHRP, cloneHRP, track, hip, origT
        local stuck, restarting = false, false
        local function add(c) conns[#conns + 1] = c end
        local function rewire(char, from, to)
            for _, v in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("Weld") or v:IsA("Motor6D") then
                    if v.Part0 == from then v.Part0 = to end
                    if v.Part1 == from then v.Part1 = to end
                end
            end
        end
        local function animTrick(char, hum)
            if not (char and hum and hum.Health > 0) then return end
            local an = Instance.new("Animation")
            an.AnimationId = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=18537363391"
            local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", hum)
            track = animator:LoadAnimation(an)
            track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
            track.Looped = true
            track:Play(0, 1, 0)
            an:Destroy()
            add(track.Stopped:Connect(function()
                if active then animTrick(char, hum) end
            end))
            task.defer(function()
                if not track then return end
                track.TimePosition = 0.7
                task.delay(0.1, function() if track then track:AdjustSpeed(math.huge) end end)
            end)
        end
        stop = function()
            active, stuck = false, false
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            conns = {}
            if origT then
                for p, v in pairs(origT) do
                    if p and p.Parent then pcall(function() p.LocalTransparencyModifier = v end) end
                end
                origT = nil
            end
            if track then
                pcall(function() track:AdjustSpeed(1) track:Stop(0) track:Destroy() end)
                track = nil
            end
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if realHRP and realHRP:IsDescendantOf(game) and hum then
                local tmp = Instance.new("Model")
                tmp.Parent = game
                char.Parent = tmp
                realHRP.Parent = char
                char.PrimaryPart = realHRP
                char.Parent = workspace
                realHRP.CanCollide = true
                rewire(char, cloneHRP, realHRP)
                if cloneHRP then
                    local at = cloneHRP.CFrame
                    cloneHRP:Destroy()
                    cloneHRP = nil
                    realHRP.CFrame = at
                end
                hum.HipHeight = hip or 0
                tmp:Destroy()
            end
            realHRP, cloneHRP = nil, nil
        end
        start = function()
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum or active then return false end
            if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then return false end
            local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(LP.Name)
            if folder then
                for _, n in ipairs({ "DoubleRig", "Constraints" }) do
                    local x = folder:FindFirstChild(n)
                    if x then x:Destroy() end
                end
            end
            for _, s in ipairs({ "Dead", "FallingDown", "Ragdoll" }) do
                hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType[s], false)
            end
            hip = hum.HipHeight
            realHRP = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not (realHRP and realHRP.Parent) then return false end
            local tmp = Instance.new("Model")
            tmp.Parent = game
            char.Parent = tmp
            cloneHRP = realHRP:Clone()
            cloneHRP.Parent = char
            realHRP.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera
            cloneHRP.CFrame = realHRP.CFrame
            char.PrimaryPart = cloneHRP
            char.Parent = workspace
            rewire(char, realHRP, cloneHRP)
            tmp:Destroy()
            active = true
            animTrick(char, hum)
            origT = {}
            for _, p in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                    origT[p] = p.LocalTransparencyModifier
                    p.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
                end
            end
            add(char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
                if not (active and desc:IsA("BasePart")) then return end
                if origT and origT[desc] == nil then origT[desc] = desc.LocalTransparencyModifier end
                pcall(function() desc.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 end)
            end))
            local lastTarget, grace, protN = nil, 60, 0
            add(RS.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
                if not active then return end
                protN += 1
                if hum.Parent and protN % 3 == 0 then
                    if hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth then
                        hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
                    end
                    hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false)
                end
                if not (realHRP and realHRP.Parent) then return end
                local base = cloneHRP
                if not (base and base.Parent) then return end
                if grace > 0 then
                    grace = grace - 1
                elseif not stuck and lastTarget
                    and (realHRP.Position - lastTarget).Magnitude > 2.25 then
                    stuck = true
                    if not restarting then
                        task.spawn(function()
                            restarting = true
                            stop()
                            task.wait(0.5)
                            restarting = false
                            start()
                        end)
                    end
                end
                if not (active and realHRP and realHRP.Parent and base.Parent) then return end
                local dd = Config.InvisibleStealDepth or 7
                local aa = Config.InvisibleStealRotation or 180
                local z = 0.01 + (math.clamp(dd, 0, 10) / 10) * 0.19
                local cf = base.CFrame - Vector3.new(0, hum.HipHeight + (base.Size.Y / 2) - 1 + z, 0)
                realHRP.CFrame = cf * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(aa), 0, 0)
                realHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = base.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                realHRP.CanCollide = false
                if realHRP.Parent ~= workspace.CurrentCamera then
                    realHRP.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera
                end
                lastTarget = cf.Position
            end))
            add(LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function() if active then stop() end end))
            return true
        end
    end

    local function tryStart()
        if Config.InvSteal == false then return end
        if _G.JAF_DropBrainrotActive and _G.JAF_DropBrainrotActive() then return end
        if active or armed then return end
        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then return end
        armed = true
        task.delay(1.2, function()
            armed = false
            if Config.InvSteal == false then return end
            if active or LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then return end
            boot()
            pcall(start)
        end)
    end
    _G.SXE_StopInvisSteal = function()
        if active and stop then pcall(stop) end
        armed = false
    end
    _G.SXE_ResetInvisSteal = function()
        armed = false
        if active and stop then pcall(stop) end
        active = false
        local char = LP.Character
        if char then
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam and not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                for _, ch in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do
                    if ch:IsA("BasePart") and ch.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
                        pcall(function() ch.Parent = char; char.PrimaryPart = ch; ch.CanCollide = true end)
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true and Config.InvSteal ~= false then
            boot()
            task.defer(function()
                if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true and Config.InvSteal ~= false then
                    pcall(start)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    _G.SXE_InvStealSet = function(on)
        Config.InvSteal = on and true or false
        if _G.SXE_RefreshControlButtons then _G.SXE_RefreshControlButtons() end
        if _G.SXE_SaveControls then _G.SXE_SaveControls() end
        if not Config.InvSteal then
            armed = false
            if active and stop then pcall(stop) end
            return
        end
        tryStart()
    end
    LP:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Stealing"):Connect(function()
        if LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") ~= true then
            armed = false
            if active and stop then pcall(stop) end
            return
        end
        tryStart()
    end)
end
]=]
if not game:IsLoaded() then
    game.Loaded:Wait()
end
do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer or Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
    if not lp.Character then
        lp.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    end
    if not workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots") then
        workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 10)
    end
end
task.spawn(function()
    pcall(__sxeStrip)
end)

-- Aggressive effect/animation/Neon stripper on all plots + RMA
task.spawn(function()
    local KILL = {
        ParticleEmitter = true, Beam = true, Trail = true,
        Fire = true, Smoke = true, Sparkles = true,
        PointLight = true, SpotLight = true, SurfaceLight = true,
        Highlight = true, Decal = true, Texture = true,
        Animator = true, AnimationController = true,
    }
    local KILL_NAME = { VfxInstance = true, _VfxInstance = true }
    local function strip(d)
        if KILL[d.ClassName] or KILL_NAME[d.Name] then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        elseif d:IsA("BasePart") and d.Material == Enum.Material.Neon then
            pcall(function() d.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic end)
        end
    end
    local hooked = {}
    local function hookFolder(folder)
        if not folder or hooked[folder] then return end
        hooked[folder] = true
        for _, d in ipairs(folder:GetDescendants()) do strip(d) end
        folder.DescendantAdded:Connect(strip)
    end
    local function scanAll()
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if plots then hookFolder(plots) end
        local rma = workspace:FindFirstChild("RenderedMovingAnimals")
        if rma then hookFolder(rma) end
    end
    scanAll()
    workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
        if ch.Name == "Plots" or ch.Name == "RenderedMovingAnimals" then
            task.wait(0.1)
            hookFolder(ch)
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(2)
            local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
            if plots then
                for _, d in ipairs(plots:GetDescendants()) do strip(d) end
            end
        end
    end)
end)

-- Remove left-side UI buttons + bottom-left elements
task.spawn(function()
    local pg = game.Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
    if not pg then return end
    local KILL_BTNS = {
        Index = true, Rebirth = true, Shop = true,
        DuelsMachinePlayerList = true, TradePlayerList = true, Codes = true,
    }
    -- ScreenGuis to destroy entirely
    local KILL_GUIS = {
        LeftBottom = true, HoneyBeeBase = true,
    }
    local function strip()
        local lc = pg:FindFirstChild("LeftCenter")
        if lc then
            for _, d in ipairs(lc:GetDescendants()) do
                if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and KILL_BTNS[d.Name] then
                    pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
        for name in pairs(KILL_GUIS) do
            local g = pg:FindFirstChild(name)
            if g then pcall(function() g:Destroy() end) end
        end
    end
    local function hookLC(lc)
        strip()
        lc.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
            if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and KILL_BTNS[d.Name] then
                pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
            end
        end)
    end
    local lc = pg:FindFirstChild("LeftCenter")
    if lc then hookLC(lc) end
    pg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(g)
        if g.Name == "LeftCenter" then hookLC(g) end
        if KILL_GUIS[g.Name] then
            pcall(function() g:Destroy() end)
        end
    end)
    pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and KILL_BTNS[d.Name] then
            pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do task.wait(2); strip() end
    end)
end)

-- Base Timer: mirrors real game timer at top of screen when inside a base
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(6)
    local LP = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    while not _G.__SXE_MyPlot do task.wait() end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return end
    local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
    local pg = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    local DETECTION_RADIUS = 55
    local gui = pg:FindFirstChild("SXEBaseTimer")
    if gui then gui:Destroy() end
    gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "SXEBaseTimer"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.DisplayOrder = 100
    gui.Parent = pg
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 60)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0, 20)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.Visible = false
    frame.Parent = gui
    local oL = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    oL.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
    oL.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    oL.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    oL.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    oL.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    oL.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    oL.TextSize = 18
    oL.Text = ""
    oL.Parent = frame
    local tL = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    tL.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0)
    tL.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
    tL.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tL.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    tL.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    tL.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    tL.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    tL.TextSize = 28
    tL.Text = ""
    tL.Parent = frame
    local REFS = { "MainRoot", "AnimalTarget", "StealHitbox", "Spawn", "DeliveryHitbox" }
    local function findPlot()
        local c = LP.Character
        local h = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not h then return nil end
        local best, bd
        for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            local md
            for _, r in ipairs(REFS) do
                local ref = p:FindFirstChild(r)
                if ref and ref:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local d = (h.Position - ref.Position).Magnitude
                    if not md or d < md then md = d end
                end
            end
            if md and md < DETECTION_RADIUS and (not bd or md < bd) then best, bd = p, md end
        end
        return best
    end
    local function getName(p)
        local s = p:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
        if s then
            for _, d in ipairs(s:GetDescendants()) do
                if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.Text and #d.Text > 0 and d.Text ~= "YOUR BASE" then
                    return d.Text
                end
            end
        end
        return "Base"
    end
    local function getTimer(p)
        local pb = p:FindFirstChild("Purchases")
        if not pb then return nil end
        pb = pb:FindFirstChild("PlotBlock")
        if not pb then return nil end
        pb = pb:FindFirstChild("Main")
        if not pb then return nil end
        pb = pb:FindFirstChild("BillboardGui")
        if not pb then return nil end
        local rt = pb:FindFirstChild("RemainingTime")
        if not rt or not rt:IsA("TextLabel") then return nil end
        return rt.Text
    end
    RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local p = findPlot()
        if p then
            local t = getTimer(p)
            if t then
                frame.Visible = true
                oL.Text = getName(p)
                tL.Text = t
            else
                frame.Visible = false
            end
        else
            frame.Visible = false
        end
    end)
end)

-- Laser limiter: 3 floor 1, 2 floor 2, 0 floor 3
task.spawn(function()
    local FLOOR_LIMITS = { 3, 2, 0 }
    local function getY(m)
        local ok, pv = pcall(function() return m:GetPivot() end)
        if ok and pv then return pv.Position.Y end
        local bp = m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        return bp and bp.Position.Y
    end
    local function getX(m)
        local bp = m:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        return bp and bp.Position.X or 0
    end
    local function limitLaser(laser)
        if not laser or not laser.Parent then return end
        local kids = laser:GetChildren()
        if #kids <= 0 then return end
        local floors = {}
        for _, ch in ipairs(kids) do
            local y = getY(ch)
            if y then
                local key
                for ey in pairs(floors) do
                    if math.abs(ey - y) <= 3 then key = ey; break end
                end
                if not key then key = y end
                if not floors[key] then floors[key] = {} end
                table.insert(floors[key], ch)
            end
        end
        local fl = {}
        for y, arr in pairs(floors) do table.insert(fl, { y = y, arr = arr }) end
        table.sort(fl, function(a, b) return a.y < b.y end)
        local keep = {}
        for i, e in ipairs(fl) do
            local kc = FLOOR_LIMITS[i] or 0
            if kc > 0 then
                table.sort(e.arr, function(a, b) return getX(a) < getX(b) end)
                local M = #e.arr
                if M <= kc then
                    for _, m in ipairs(e.arr) do keep[m] = true end
                else
                    local s = math.floor((M - kc) / 2) + 1
                    for j = s, s + kc - 1 do
                        if e.arr[j] then keep[e.arr[j]] = true end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, ch in ipairs(kids) do
            if not keep[ch] then pcall(function() ch:Destroy() end) end
        end
    end
    local hooked = {}
    local function hookAndLimit(plot)
        local l = plot:FindFirstChild("Laser")
        if l then
            limitLaser(l)
            if not hooked[l] then
                hooked[l] = true
                l.ChildAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(); limitLaser(l) end)
            end
        end
        if not hooked[plot] then
            hooked[plot] = true
            plot.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
                if ch.Name == "Laser" then
                    task.wait()
                    limitLaser(ch)
                    if not hooked[ch] then
                        hooked[ch] = true
                        ch.ChildAdded:Connect(function() task.wait(); limitLaser(ch) end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    -- Immediate scan
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if plots then
        for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do hookAndLimit(p) end
        plots.ChildAdded:Connect(function(p) task.wait(); hookAndLimit(p) end)
    else
        workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(ch)
            if ch.Name == "Plots" then
                for _, p in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do hookAndLimit(p) end
                ch.ChildAdded:Connect(function(p) task.wait(); hookAndLimit(p) end)
            end
        end)
    end
    -- Fast sweep: rapide au debut, lent ensuite pour + de FPS
    local _t0 = os.clock()
    while true do
        task.wait((os.clock() - _t0) < 8 and 0.3 or 2)
        local ps = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if ps then
            for _, p in ipairs(ps:GetChildren()) do
                local l = p:FindFirstChild("Laser")
                if l then limitLaser(l) end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- Steal speed boost: 28 studs/sec during stealing
task.spawn(function()
    while not Config do task.wait(0.2) end
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local conn, last = nil, false
    while true do
        task.wait(0.3)
        local on = LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true and Config.StealSpeed == true
        if on == last then continue end
        last = on
        if on then
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                local c = LP.Character; if not c then return end
                local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local hr = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not h or not hr or h.Health <= 0 then return end
                local md = h.MoveDirection
                if md.Magnitude > 0 and 28 > h.WalkSpeed then
                    hr.CFrame = hr.CFrame + (md * (28 - h.WalkSpeed) * (dt or 0.016))
                end
            end)
        else
            if conn then conn:Disconnect(); conn = nil end
        end
    end
end)

task.defer(function()
    local fn = loadstring(SRC)
    if not fn then
        return
    end
    fn()
end)

-- Extracted kick function from zizi1.lua
local function psCode(link)
    if type(link) ~= "string" then return nil end
    return link:match("[?&]linkCode=([^&]+)")
        or link:match("[?&]code=([^&]+)")
end

local _kickLocalPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local function kickOut()
    if _G.MeerkoKickToPS == true then
        local code = psCode(_G.MeerkoPrivateServerLink)
        if code and code ~= "" then
            local ok = pcall(function()
                game:GetService("ExperienceService"):LaunchExperience({
                    placeId = tonumber(_G.MeerkoPrivateServerPlaceId) or game.PlaceId,
                    linkCode = code,
                })
            end)
            if ok then return end
        end
    end
    if pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end) then return end
    pcall(function() _kickLocalPlayer:Kick("") end)
end
_G.MeerkoKickOut = kickOut

-- Kick hotkey: F
local _kickUserInput = game:GetService("UserInputService")
if _G.MeerkoKickKeyConnection then
    pcall(function() _G.MeerkoKickKeyConnection:Disconnect() end)
end
_G.MeerkoKickKeyConnection = _kickUserInput.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed or _G.SXE_KeybindActionsActive or input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.F then return end
    if _kickUserInput:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    if _G.MeerkoKickOut then pcall(_G.MeerkoKickOut) end
end)

-- Keybind & Actions from Extras 2; scoped independently of the teleport engine.
do
do
    local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local FILE = "BPVPRIV_KeybindActions.json"
    local data
    local function load()
        if data then return data end
        data = {}
        if readfile then
            pcall(function()
                local raw = readfile(FILE)
                local t = HS:JSONDecode(raw)
                if type(t) == "table" then data = t end
            end)
        end
        return data
    end
    _G.SXE_ActionsCfg = {
        get = function(section)
            local d = load()
            if type(d[section]) ~= "table" then d[section] = {} end
            return d[section]
        end,
        save = function()
            if not writefile then return end
            pcall(function() writefile(FILE, HS:JSONEncode(load())) end)
        end,
    }
end

_G.SXE_ActionsUI = nil
if not _G.SXE_ActionsUI then
    local UI = {}
    local _Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local _UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local _TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local _RS = game:GetService("RunService")

    UI.C = {
        BG      = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
        PANEL   = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
        ROW     = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28),
        ROW_HI  = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50),
        BTN     = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 28),
        BTN_ON  = Color3.fromRGB(36, 110, 64),
        ACCENT  = Color3.fromRGB(36, 110, 64),
        ACCENT2 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 200),
        TRACK   = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50),
        TEXT    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        MUTED   = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 165),
        DANGER  = Color3.fromRGB(230, 70, 90),
        STROKE  = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75),
    }
    UI.FONT  = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    UI.FONTB = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    UI.FONTK = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    UI.ROW_H = 44
    UI.BTN_H = 26
    UI.PAD   = 8

    function UI.corner(inst, r)
        local c = Instance.new("UICorner"); c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 8); c.Parent = inst; return c
    end
    function UI.stroke(inst, color, t)
        local s = Instance.new("UIStroke"); s.Color = color or UI.C.STROKE; s.Thickness = t or 1
        s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border; s.Parent = inst; return s
    end

    function UI.screen(name, displayOrder)
        local parent = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") or _Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        pcall(function()
            local old = parent:FindFirstChild(name); if old then old:Destroy() end
            local pg = _Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            local old2 = pg and pg:FindFirstChild(name); if old2 then old2:Destroy() end
        end)
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = name; sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        sg.DisplayOrder = displayOrder or 999999
        sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        pcall(function() sg.Parent = parent end)
        if not sg.Parent then sg.Parent = _Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
        return sg
    end

    function UI.drag(handle, target, onDrop)
        local dragging, dStart, pStart
        handle.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true; dStart = i.Position; pStart = target.Position
            end
        end)
        handle.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if dragging and onDrop then pcall(onDrop, target) end
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
        _UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if not dragging then return end
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local d = i.Position - dStart
                target.Position = UDim2.new(pStart.X.Scale, pStart.X.Offset + d.X, pStart.Y.Scale, pStart.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end)
    end

    function UI.panel(opts)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Name = opts.name or "Panel"; f.Active = true; f.BorderSizePixel = 0
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(opts.w or 260, opts.h or 300)
        f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(opts.x or 200, opts.y or 200)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.PANEL
        f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
        UI.corner(f, 10); UI.stroke(f)
        f.Parent = opts.parent

        local t = Instance.new("TextButton", f)
        t.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32); t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Text = opts.title or ""; t.Font = UI.FONTK; t.TextSize = 13
        t.TextColor3 = UI.C.TEXT; t.AutoButtonColor = false; t.Active = true

        local bar = Instance.new("Frame", f)
        bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 2); bar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -17, 0, 30)
        bar.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.ACCENT; bar.BorderSizePixel = 0; bar.Visible = false
        UI.corner(bar, 2)

        if opts.closable then
            local x = Instance.new("TextButton", f)
            x.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22); x.Position = UDim2.new(1, -27, 0, 5)
            x.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.BTN; x.Text = "x"; x.Font = UI.FONTB; x.TextSize = 12
            x.TextColor3 = UI.C.MUTED; x.AutoButtonColor = false
            UI.corner(x, 6)
            x.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if opts.onClose then opts.onClose() else f.Visible = false end
            end)
        end

        local body = Instance.new("Frame", f)
        body.Name = "Body"; body.BackgroundTransparency = 1; body.BorderSizePixel = 0
        body.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 36); body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -36)

        UI.drag(t, f, opts.onDrop)
        return f, body, t
    end

    function UI.button(opts)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * UI.PAD, 0, opts.h or UI.BTN_H)
        b.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(UI.PAD, opts.y or 0)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = opts.accent and UI.C.ACCENT or UI.C.BTN
        b.Text = opts.text or ""; b.Font = UI.FONTB; b.TextSize = 11
        b.TextColor3 = UI.C.TEXT; b.BorderSizePixel = 0; b.AutoButtonColor = false
        UI.corner(b, 7)
        b.Parent = opts.parent
        local function setOn(on)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = on and UI.C.BTN_ON or UI.C.BTN
        end
        if opts.on ~= nil then setOn(opts.on) end
        b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() _TS:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.15 }):Play() end)
        b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() _TS:Create(b, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play() end)
        if opts.onClick then b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(opts.onClick) end
        return b, setOn
    end

    function UI.smallButton(opts)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(opts.w or 28, opts.h or 18)
        b.Position = opts.pos or UDim2.fromOffset(opts.x or 0, opts.y or 0)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.BTN; b.Text = opts.text or ""
        b.Font = UI.FONTB; b.TextSize = opts.textSize or 12
        b.TextColor3 = UI.C.TEXT; b.BorderSizePixel = 0; b.AutoButtonColor = false
        UI.corner(b, 6)
        b.Parent = opts.parent
        if opts.onClick then b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(opts.onClick) end
        return b
    end

    function UI.label(opts)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1; l.BorderSizePixel = 0
        l.Size = opts.size2 or UDim2.new(1, -(opts.w and 0 or 2 * UI.PAD), 0, opts.h or 18)
        if opts.w then l.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(opts.w, opts.h or 18) end
        l.Position = opts.pos or UDim2.fromOffset(opts.x or UI.PAD, opts.y or 0)
        l.Text = opts.text or ""; l.Font = opts.bold and UI.FONTB or UI.FONT
        l.TextSize = opts.size or 11
        l.TextColor3 = opts.color or (opts.muted and UI.C.MUTED or UI.C.TEXT)
        l.TextXAlignment = opts.align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        l.Parent = opts.parent
        return l
    end

    function UI.row(opts)
        local r = Instance.new("Frame")
        r.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * UI.PAD, 0, opts.h or UI.ROW_H)
        r.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(UI.PAD, opts.y or 0)
        r.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.ROW; r.BorderSizePixel = 0
        UI.corner(r, 7)
        local st = UI.stroke(r, UI.C.ROW, 1)
        r.Parent = opts.parent
        local function setSelected(on)
            r.BackgroundColor3 = on and UI.C.ROW_HI or UI.C.ROW
            st.Color = on and UI.C.ACCENT or UI.C.ROW
        end
        if opts.selected then setSelected(true) end
        return r, setSelected
    end

    function UI.scroll(opts)
        local s = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        s.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.BG; s.BorderSizePixel = 0
        s.Position = UDim2.new(0, UI.PAD - 2, 0, opts.y or 0)
        s.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * (UI.PAD - 2), 1, -((opts.y or 0) + (opts.bottom or 6)))
        s.ScrollBarThickness = 3; s.ScrollBarImageColor3 = UI.C.ACCENT
        s.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        s.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        UI.corner(s, 8)
        s.Parent = opts.parent
        return s
    end

    function UI.slider(opts)
        local min, max, step = opts.min, opts.max, opts.step or 1
        local row = Instance.new("Frame")
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2 * UI.PAD, 0, 42); row.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(UI.PAD, opts.y or 0)
        row.BackgroundTransparency = 1; row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.Parent = opts.parent

        local lbl = UI.label({ parent = row, x = 2, y = 0, h = 18, bold = true, size = 11 })
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0, 18)

        local track = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
        track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 10); track.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 26)
        track.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.TRACK; track.Text = ""; track.AutoButtonColor = false
        track.BorderSizePixel = 0; track.Active = true
        UI.corner(track, 5)
        local fill = Instance.new("Frame", track)
        fill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0); fill.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.ACCENT; fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        UI.corner(fill, 5)
        local knob = Instance.new("Frame", track)
        knob.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14); knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.TEXT; knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        UI.corner(knob, 7)

        local function fmt(v)
            if step < 1 then return string.format("%.2f", v) end
            return tostring(math.floor(v + 0.5))
        end
        local function refresh()
            local v = math.clamp(tonumber(opts.get()) or min, min, max)
            local rel = (v - min) / math.max(max - min, 1e-6)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 1, 0)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(rel, 0, 0.5, 0)
            lbl.Text = opts.label .. "  " .. fmt(v)
        end
        local function applyAt(x, final)
            local rel = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
            local v = min + (max - min) * rel
            v = math.clamp(math.floor(v / step + 0.5) * step, min, max)
            opts.set(v); refresh()
            if opts.onChange then opts.onChange(v, final) end
        end
        local sliding = false
        track.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                sliding = true; applyAt(i.Position.X, false)
            end
        end)
        _UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if not sliding then return end
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                applyAt(i.Position.X, false)
            end
        end)
        _UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if sliding then
                    sliding = false
                    local v = tonumber(opts.get()) or min
                    if opts.onChange then opts.onChange(v, true) end
                end
            end
        end)
        local function bump(dir)
            local v = math.clamp((tonumber(opts.get()) or min) + dir * step, min, max)
            opts.set(v); refresh()
            if opts.onChange then opts.onChange(v, true) end
        end
        UI.smallButton({ parent = row, pos = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 0), w = 28, h = 18, text = "-", onClick = function() bump(-1) end })
        UI.smallButton({ parent = row, pos = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 0), w = 28, h = 18, text = "+", onClick = function() bump(1) end })
        refresh()
        return row, refresh
    end

    function UI.textbox(opts)
        local tb = Instance.new("TextBox")
        tb.Size = opts.size2 or UDim2.new(1, -2 * UI.PAD, 0, opts.h or 26)
        tb.Position = opts.pos or UDim2.fromOffset(opts.x or UI.PAD, opts.y or 0)
        tb.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.BG; tb.BorderSizePixel = 0
        tb.Font = UI.FONT; tb.TextSize = 11; tb.TextColor3 = UI.C.TEXT
        tb.PlaceholderText = opts.placeholder or ""; tb.PlaceholderColor3 = UI.C.MUTED
        tb.Text = opts.text or ""; tb.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        tb.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        UI.corner(tb, 7); UI.stroke(tb)
        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", tb); pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        tb.Parent = opts.parent
        return tb
    end

    do
        local sg, holder
        local function ensure()
            if sg and sg.Parent then return end
            sg = UI.screen("MynxxNotif", 1000000)
            holder = Instance.new("Frame", sg)
            holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1; holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
            holder.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1)
            holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16); holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 300)
            local lay = Instance.new("UIListLayout", holder)
            lay.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            lay.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom
            lay.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
            lay.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
        end
        local order = 0
        function UI.notify(title, text, isError)
            ensure()
            if #holder:GetChildren() > 6 then
                for _, c in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
                    if c:IsA("Frame") then c:Destroy(); break end
                end
            end
            order += 1
            local card = Instance.new("Frame")
            card.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(260, 46); card.BackgroundColor3 = UI.C.PANEL
            card.BorderSizePixel = 0; card.LayoutOrder = order; card.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            UI.corner(card, 8); UI.stroke(card, isError and UI.C.DANGER or UI.C.STROKE)
            local bar = Instance.new("Frame", card)
            bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -12); bar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6)
            bar.BackgroundColor3 = isError and UI.C.DANGER or UI.C.ACCENT; bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
            UI.corner(bar, 2)
            local a = UI.label({ parent = card, x = 16, y = 6, h = 16, bold = true, size = 11, text = tostring(title or "") })
            a.Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 16)
            local b = UI.label({ parent = card, x = 16, y = 23, h = 16, size = 11, muted = true, text = tostring(text or "") })
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 16)
            card.Parent = holder
            _TS:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }):Play()
            task.delay(3, function()
                if not card.Parent then return end
                local tw = _TS:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.2), { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
                tw:Play(); tw.Completed:Wait()
                if card.Parent then card:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
    end

    _G.SXE_ActionsUI = UI
end
local UI = _G.SXE_ActionsUI

local function nowSpawn(fn)
    task.spawn(function()
        while not game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer do task.wait() end
        fn()
    end)
end
local stagSpawn = nowSpawn
nowSpawn(function()
    local Players=game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService=game:GetService("RunService")
    local LP=Players.LocalPlayer
    local refreshUI
    local WS = { enabled = false, conn = nil }
    local function setWalkSpeedEnabled(en)
        WS.enabled = en
        if WS.conn then WS.conn:Disconnect(); WS.conn = nil end
        if refreshUI then refreshUI() end
        if not en then return end
        WS.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if _G.FlingActive then return end
            local c = LP.Character
            local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

            local target = tonumber(_G.MynxxWSValue) or 28
            if hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 and target > hum.WalkSpeed then
                root.CFrame = root.CFrame + (hum.MoveDirection * (target - hum.WalkSpeed) * dt)
            end
        end)
    end
    _G.setWalkSpeedEnabled = setWalkSpeedEnabled

    local function setWalkSpeedValue(v)
        v = math.clamp(math.floor((tonumber(v) or 28) + 0.5), 15, 29)
        _G.MynxxWSValue = v
        return v
    end
    _G.setWalkSpeedValue = setWalkSpeedValue
    setWalkSpeedValue(_G.MynxxWSValue)


end)
_G.toggleInvisibleSteal = function()
    local cfg = _G.SXE_Message40Config
    if not cfg then return end
    local on = cfg.InvSteal == false
    if _G.SXE_InvStealSet then _G.SXE_InvStealSet(on) else cfg.InvSteal = on end
end
nowSpawn(function()
local LP = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local FLING_POWER   = 99999999
    local FLING_SUSTAIN = 0.15
    local function flingUp()
        local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        local hrp  = char and (char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
        if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        _G.FlingActive = true

        pcall(function()
            hum.Sit = false
            hum.PlatformStand = true
            if hrp.Anchored then hrp.Anchored = false end
        end)

        local up = Vector3.new(0, FLING_POWER, 0)
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while os.clock() - t0 < FLING_SUSTAIN do
            char = LP.Character
            hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            hrp  = char and (char.PrimaryPart or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
            if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then break end
            if hrp.Anchored then hrp.Anchored = false end
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = up
            hrp.Velocity = up
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end

        pcall(function()
            if hum and hum.Parent then
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
            end
        end)
        _G.FlingActive = false
    end
    _G.FlingUp = flingUp

end)
nowSpawn(function()
local okJQ, errJQ = pcall(function()
    local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
    local Players         = game:GetService("Players")
    local hasES, ExperienceService = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ExperienceService") end)
    if not hasES then ExperienceService = nil end

    local MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5
    local RETRY_DELAY  = 3
    local MIN_INTERVAL = 3
    local RAW_INTERVAL = 2

    local enabled = false
    local hooked  = false
    local bypass  = false
    local launchGen = 0
    local activeJobId = nil
    local lastAttemptAt = 0
    local lastRawAt = 0
    local oldNamecall
    local oldTeleportToPlaceInstance

    local function setStatus(text, isError)
        if type(_G.MynxxJoinQueueStatus) == "function" then
            pcall(_G.MynxxJoinQueueStatus, tostring(text), isError and true or false)
        end
    end

    local function cleanJobId(jobId)
        jobId = tostring(jobId or "")
        return (jobId:gsub("[%s\"'{}%[%]]", ""))
    end

    local function shouldDropRaw()
        if os.clock() - lastRawAt < RAW_INTERVAL then return true end
        lastRawAt = os.clock()
        return false
    end

    local function rawTeleport(placeId, jobId)
        bypass = true
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            if oldTeleportToPlaceInstance then
                oldTeleportToPlaceInstance(TeleportService, placeId, jobId, Players.LocalPlayer)
            else
                TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, jobId, Players.LocalPlayer)
            end
        end)
        bypass = false
        return ok, err
    end

    local function attemptOnce(placeId, jobId)
        if ExperienceService then
            return pcall(function()
                return ExperienceService:LaunchExperience({ placeId = placeId, gameInstanceId = jobId })
            end)
        end
        return rawTeleport(placeId, jobId)
    end

    local function launch(placeId, jobId)
        placeId = tonumber(placeId)
        jobId = cleanJobId(jobId)
        if not placeId or placeId == 0 then placeId = game.PlaceId end
        if jobId == "" then setStatus("No JobId given", true); return false end

        if jobId == activeJobId then return true end
        activeJobId = jobId
        launchGen = launchGen + 1
        local myGen = launchGen
        task.spawn(function()
            for attempt = 1, MAX_ATTEMPTS do
                if launchGen ~= myGen then return end
                local cool = MIN_INTERVAL - (os.clock() - lastAttemptAt)
                if cool > 0 then task.wait(cool) end
                if launchGen ~= myGen then return end
                lastAttemptAt = os.clock()
                setStatus(("Launching... (%d/%d)"):format(attempt, MAX_ATTEMPTS), false)
                local ok, err = attemptOnce(placeId, jobId)
                if ok then setStatus("Queued / launching", false); return end
                warn("[JoinQueue] attempt " .. attempt .. " failed: " .. tostring(err))
                setStatus(tostring(err), true)
                task.wait(RETRY_DELAY)
            end
            if launchGen ~= myGen then return end
            setStatus("Gave up, waiting next id", true)
            activeJobId = nil
        end)
        return true
    end

    TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed:Connect(function(_, result, message)
        warn("[JoinQueue] teleport failed: " .. tostring(result) .. " - " .. tostring(message))
        setStatus(tostring(result) .. ": " .. tostring(message), true)
    end)

    local function installHook()
        if hooked then return end
        hooked = true
        if hookfunction then
            oldTeleportToPlaceInstance = hookfunction(
                TeleportService.TeleportToPlaceInstance,
                newcclosure(function(self, placeId, jobId, ...)

                    if enabled and not bypass then launch(placeId, jobId); return end
                    return oldTeleportToPlaceInstance(self, placeId, jobId, ...)
                end)
            )
        end
        if hookmetamethod and getnamecallmethod then
            oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", newcclosure(function(self, ...)
                local method = getnamecallmethod()
                if enabled and not bypass and self == TeleportService
                    and (method == "TeleportToPlaceInstance" or method == "TeleportAsync") then
                    local args = { ... }
                    if method == "TeleportToPlaceInstance" then
                        launch(args[1], args[2]); return
                    end
                    local options = args[3]
                    local jobId = options and options.ServerInstanceId
                    if jobId and jobId ~= "" then launch(args[1], jobId); return end
                end
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end))
        end
    end

    _G.MynxxJoinQueueEnabled = false
    local function setEnabled(state)
        enabled = state and true or false
        _G.MynxxJoinQueueEnabled = enabled
        if enabled then

            installHook()
        else
            launchGen = launchGen + 1; activeJobId = nil
        end
    end
    _G.MynxxSetJoinQueue = setEnabled
    _G.MynxxToggleJoinQueue = function() setEnabled(not enabled); return enabled end

end)
if not okJQ then warn("[JOINQUEUE] BLOC EN ERREUR: " .. tostring(errJQ)) end
end)
nowSpawn(function()
local okAC, errAC = pcall(function()
    local Players    = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local UIS        = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local LP         = Players.LocalPlayer
    local PG         = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    local guiParent  = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") or PG

    local MINV, MAXV, DEFV = 50, 120, 70
    local cfg = _G.SXE_ActionsCfg.get("actions")
    if _G.MynxxFOV        == nil then _G.MynxxFOV        = DEFV end
    if _G.MynxxFOVEnabled == nil then _G.MynxxFOVEnabled = true end
    local panelX, panelY = 240, 470
    do
        if tonumber(cfg.fov) then _G.MynxxFOV = math.clamp(tonumber(cfg.fov), MINV, MAXV) end
        if tonumber(cfg.x)   then panelX = tonumber(cfg.x) end
        if tonumber(cfg.y)   then panelY = tonumber(cfg.y) end
    end
    local function saveCfg()
        cfg.fov = _G.MynxxFOV
        cfg.x = panelX; cfg.y = panelY
        _G.SXE_ActionsCfg.save()
    end

    local function setFOVValue(v)
        v = math.clamp(math.floor((tonumber(v) or DEFV) + 0.5), MINV, MAXV)
        _G.MynxxFOV = v
        pcall(function() cfg.fov = v; _G.SXE_ActionsCfg.save() end)
        return v
    end
    _G.setFOV = setFOVValue
    setFOVValue(_G.MynxxFOV)

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not _G.MynxxFOVEnabled then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            local target = tonumber(_G.MynxxFOV) or DEFV
            if math.abs(cam.FieldOfView - target) > 0.01 then
                cam.FieldOfView = target
            end
        end
    end)

end)

if not okAC then warn("[ACTIONS] BLOC EN ERREUR: " .. tostring(errAC)) end
end)
nowSpawn(function()
    local LocalPlayer = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Workspace = workspace
    local function SAB_LIVE() return LocalPlayer.Parent ~= nil end
do
    local _dropActive  = false
    local _DROP_MIN    = 0.15
    local _DROP_MAX    = 6.0
    local _INVIS_WAIT  = 2.0
    local _DROP_BASE_MAX = 200
    local _dropRestore, _dropRestoreRoot
    local _dropRoot; _dropRoot = function(char) 
        if not char then return nil end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local r = hum and hum.RootPart
        if r and r.Parent then return r end
        r = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        return (r and r.Parent) and r or nil
    end
    local function _invisOrphan() 
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return false end
        for _, c in ipairs(cam:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("BasePart") and c.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then return true end end
        return false
    end
    local _stealing; _stealing = function() 
        return LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") == true
    end
    local _flingLoop; _flingLoop = (function(char0) 
        local t0, movel = os.clock(), 0.1
        while _dropActive do
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            if not SAB_LIVE() then break end
            local el = os.clock() - t0
            if el >= _DROP_MIN and not _stealing() then break end
            if el > _DROP_MAX then _G.JAF_DropRecToken = nil; break end
            if LocalPlayer.Character ~= char0 then _G.JAF_DropRecToken = nil; break end
            if _G.JAF_DropSuppressOnce == false then
                _G.JAF_HitRecCooldownUntil = tick() + 3
            else
                _G.JAF_DropRecToken = tick() + (tonumber(_G.JAF_DropRecTokenTail) or 2)
            end
            local root = _dropRoot(char0)
            if not (char0.Parent and root) then break end
            local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if vel.Magnitude > _DROP_BASE_MAX then vel = Vector3.zero end
            _dropRestore, _dropRestoreRoot = vel, root
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel * 10000 + Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            if char0.Parent and root.Parent then root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel end
            RunService.Stepped:Wait()
            if char0.Parent and root.Parent then root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = vel + Vector3.new(0, movel, 0) movel = -movel end
        end
        _dropRestoreRoot = nil
    end)
    _G.JAF_DropBrainrot = function() 
        if _dropActive then return false end
        if _G.JAF_DropBrainrotEnabled == false then return false end
        if not SAB_LIVE() then return false end
        if _G.__SXE_TPStarted or _G.__SXE_VelFlying or _G.JAF_ResetStowActive then return false end
        _dropActive = true
        task.spawn(function() 
            local diedConn, safetyConn
            pcall(function() 
                if _invisOrphan() or _G.invisibleStealEnabled == true then
                    if _G.SXE_StopInvisSteal then pcall(_G.SXE_StopInvisSteal) end
                    _G.invisAutoActive = false
                    local _iw = os.clock()
                    while os.clock() - _iw < _INVIS_WAIT do
                        if _G.invisibleStealEnabled ~= true and not _invisOrphan() and _dropRoot(LocalPlayer.Character) then break end
                        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    end end
                if _invisOrphan() then return end
                local char0 = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum0  = char0 and char0:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum0 then diedConn = hum0.Died:Connect(function() 
_dropActive = false end) end
                if _G.JAF_DropSuppressOnce == false then
                    _G.JAF_HitRecCooldownUntil = tick() + 3
                else
                    _G.JAF_DropRecToken = tick() + (tonumber(_G.JAF_DropRecTokenTail) or 2)
                end
                _G.JAF_DropFlingActive = true
                safetyConn = RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function() 
                    local _r = _dropRestoreRoot
                    if not _r or not _r.Parent then return end
                    local v = _r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local cap = tonumber(_G.JAF_DropFlingVelCap) or 500
                    if v.Magnitude > cap then _r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _dropRestore or Vector3.zero end
                end)
                _flingLoop(char0)
            end)
            _G.JAF_DropFlingActive = false
            _dropActive = false
            if diedConn then pcall(function() 
diedConn:Disconnect() end) end
            if safetyConn then pcall(function() 
safetyConn:Disconnect() end) end
            _dropRestore, _dropRestoreRoot = nil, nil
            local _r = _dropRoot(LocalPlayer.Character)
            if _r then pcall(function() 
_r.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end) end
        end)
        return true
    end
    _G.JAF_DropBrainrotActive = function() 
return _dropActive end
end

    local dropMotor = _G.JAF_DropBrainrot
    _G.MynxxDropBrainrot = function()
        local started = dropMotor()
        if started then
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.JAF_DropBrainrotActive() do RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end
                if _G.SXE_Message40Config and _G.SXE_Message40Config.InvSteal ~= false
                    and LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("Stealing") and _G.SXE_ResetInvisSteal then
                    pcall(_G.SXE_ResetInvisSteal)
                end
            end)
        end
        return started
    end
    _G.MynxxKick = function() if _G.MeerkoKickOut then return _G.MeerkoKickOut() end LocalPlayer:Kick("") end
end)

nowSpawn(function()
local okAB, errAB = pcall(function()
    local Players            = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService         = game:GetService("RunService")
    local ReplicatedStorage  = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local Workspace          = workspace
    local LocalPlayer        = Players.LocalPlayer
    if _G.MynxxAutoBuyRange == nil then _G.MynxxAutoBuyRange = 60 end
    if _G.MynxxAutoBuyFireRange == nil then _G.MynxxAutoBuyFireRange = 150 end

    local Config = setmetatable({
        AutoBuyEnabled = false, AutoGrabSpeed = 17, AutoKickOnSteal = false, TpSettings = {},
    }, { __index = function(_, k)
        if k == "AutoBuyRange" then return tonumber(_G.MynxxAutoBuyRange) or 60 end
        return nil
    end })
    local function saveConfig() end
    local function setToggle() end
    local function ShowNotification() end
    local SharedState = { ConveyorAnimals = {} }
    local function LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(fn) return fn end

    local function kickPlayer()
        if _G.MynxxDoAutoKick then pcall(_G.MynxxDoAutoKick)
        elseif _G.MynxxKick then pcall(_G.MynxxKick) end
    end
    local CarpetState = nil
    local function setCarpetSpeed() end
    local AnimalsData, AnimalsShared, NumberUtils

    local toggleAutoBuy

    local function _initAutoBuyScope()
    local autoBuyActive = false

    pcall(function()
        local e = Workspace:FindFirstChild("XiAutoBuyRing")
        if e then e:Destroy() end
    end)

    toggleAutoBuy = function(on)
        if on ~= nil then
            autoBuyActive = on
        else
            autoBuyActive = not autoBuyActive
        end
        Config.AutoBuyEnabled = autoBuyActive
        pcall(saveConfig)
        pcall(setToggle, "Auto Buy", autoBuyActive)

        if not autoBuyActive then
            pcall(function() local e = Workspace:FindFirstChild("XiAutoBuyRing"); if e then e:Destroy() end end)
        end
        pcall(ShowNotification, "AUTO BUY", autoBuyActive and "ENABLED" or "DISABLED")
        if _G.AutoBuyOnToggle then
            pcall(_G.AutoBuyOnToggle, autoBuyActive)
        end
    end

    local RARITY_WORDS = {
        common = true, uncommon = true, rare = true, epic = true,
        legendary = true, secret = true, divine = true, rainbow = true,
        cursed = true, gold = true, diamond = true,
    }

    local function getBrainrotName(model)
        if not model then return "Brainrot", "" end
        local nameFound, genFound = "", ""
        for _, bb in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if bb:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                for _, lbl in ipairs(bb:GetDescendants()) do
                    if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") and lbl.Text and lbl.Text ~= "" then
                        local t = lbl.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                        local tl = t:lower()
                        if RARITY_WORDS[tl] then continue end
                        if t:match("^%$[%d%.]+[KkMmBb]?/s$") then
